**Latest final source-closure and README continuation:** the scoped renderer/image/font/SIMD/runtime/server/binding source audit has no actionable `todo!`, `unimplemented!`, immediate-renderer delegation, or legacy-renderer delegation markers. Active scalar/reference paths and explicit unsupported codec/output-format capabilities are source-complete policies rather than missing implementations. The root README now leads with a reserved, unpopulated post-closure benchmark table and documents actual source-built requirements, component ownership, Rust/CLI/server/C/Python/WASM/.NET/Java usage, render contracts, caller-owned surfaces, progressive revision, cache invalidation, OCR, SIMD, color management, and verification boundaries without carrying forward stale benchmark claims.
**Latest progressive live render-contract revision continuation:** `ProgressiveRenderJob::revise_render_contract` now applies the full schema-v1 contract rather than changing identity strings only. It rebuilds contract-dependent output geometry and scheduling, clears completed/cache state, invalidates prior publication identities, and preserves lifecycle state. Server, C, Python, WASM/TypeScript, .NET, and Java expose the operation; focused engine, server, and C ABI tests verify contract fingerprints, tile-grid replacement, obsolete-publication rejection, and clipped-output equivalence.
**Latest progressive render-contract parity continuation:** progressive render jobs now use the canonical schema-v1 render contract as their source identity instead of only page, DPI, and legacy render-mode labels. `RenderContract::with_render_tile` preserves base policy while deriving tile-local clip/size/stride, `ContentEngine::progressive_render_job_with_contract*` exposes Rust core entry points, `ProgressiveRenderJob` stores the normalized base contract, preserves caller-supplied clipped output regions, schedules/publishes tiles from contract-derived fingerprints, and assembles clipped output at clip-relative offsets. The page renderer has a contract-aware progressive tile renderer with proof, color, halftone, optional-content, budget, backend, and exactness policy. Retained-tile fallback events derive Standard versus high-quality availability from `ExactnessPolicy`, not `RenderMode`. Server, C ABI/header, Python, WASM/TypeScript, .NET, and Java source surfaces expose contract-backed progressive constructors. External runtime binding matrices remain deferred.
**Latest progressive retained-tile refusal event continuation:** progressive retained display-list tile failures now record `unsupported_display_list_retained_tile_refusal` in the production fallback-event report before entering `Failed`. The path remains typed refusal with no tile publication and no immediate-render fallback; the report now carries call-site, trigger, output, degradation, availability, replacement, final-policy, and tile identity fields for the failed retained tile. Evidence: focused progressive refusal tests, fallback-report serialization test, fmt check, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest PostScript exact shading-domain regionalization continuation:** SVG/PS vector fallback now carries axial/radial shading `/Domain` values into vector-shading records, and PostScript exact Type 2/Type 3 function sidecars can emit regional `shfill` output for finite non-unit shading domains. PostScript emits `/Domain [...]` only for exact sidecars; sampled gradients retain normalized stop behavior, and nonlinear SVG output remains typed refusal because SVG has no exact function representation. Evidence: focused PostScript shading-domain tests, fmt check, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest ICC transform render-contract cache-scope/proof-cache/byte-accounting continuation:** `ColorTransformOptions.cache_scope` is populated from the active schema-v1 render-contract fingerprint in page rendering and included in the bounded ICC transform cache key. The key also carries a transform kind so ordinary ICCBased-to-sRGB, built-in sRGB proof probes, and native output-intent proof transforms cannot alias. Display, print, proof, backend, intent, and output-profile policy variants therefore do not share cached ICC transforms unless the render-contract scope is identical. Native LittleCMS output-intent proofing now uses the same bounded cache and metrics path. ICC cache entries now carry byte costs, admission/rejection counters, byte-pressure eviction, hit-order LRU refresh, and public `ColorReport` byte-limit telemetry. Standalone CMM helper calls keep deterministic scope `0`. Evidence: `transform_cache_key_includes_render_contract_scope`, `color_transform_options_include_render_contract_scope`, `output_intent_proof_transform_uses_bounded_cache`, `transform_cache_rejects_entries_over_byte_budget`, `transform_cache_hit_refreshes_lru_eviction_order`, focused default/native tests, fmt check, engine check, default/native clippy, and diff check.
**Latest font source-marker cache pruning continuation:** document font byte/resolver cache keys now carry `font:ref:{object}:{generation}:` markers for preserved page/Form font resource refs and nested indirect refs inside font dictionaries. Source-scoped cache pruning removes matching font byte and resolver entries when a mapped font, FontDescriptor, FontFile, or descendant source object changes, while unrelated font cache entries remain admitted. Evidence: `document_font_cache_key_includes_source_reference_markers`, `source_scoped_font_cache_pruning_uses_source_reference_markers`, `cargo fmt --all`, focused `font_cache` tests, fmt check, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest image decode tile/component write-set invalidation continuation:** transaction cache-plan envelopes now normalize source-region, image-region, region-decode, reduction, image-component, component-selection, codec-tile, image-tile, JPX-tile, JPX-component, progressive-image, and image-progression refs through snake_case and binding-style camelCase fields. Known structured object/generation refs map through `RenderDocumentCache` source identities, register `image-decode:ref:{object}:{generation}:` source-cache markers, and invalidate exact dependent tiles; unknown refs still conservatively reset. This is invalidation/ref-normalization source work, not a claim that codec-native ROI/progressive JPX output is complete. Evidence: `nested_write_set_collector_accepts_image_decode_parameter_aliases`, `render_invalidation_plan_json_maps_image_decode_tile_component_refs`, `cargo fmt --all`, focused image-decode alias tests, fmt check, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest optional-content Properties write-set invalidation continuation:** transaction cache-plan envelopes now normalize OC `/Properties` dirty refs through both snake_case and binding-style camelCase fields: `changed_oc_properties_refs` / `changedOCPropertiesRefs` and `changed_properties_refs` / `changedPropertiesRefs`. Known structured object/generation refs map through `RenderDocumentCache` source identities, register `properties:ref:{object}:{generation}:` source-cache markers, and invalidate only the exact dependent tiles; unknown refs still conservatively reset. **Latest image/shading/pattern/SMask/Properties dependency/accounting continuation:** backend-plan resource arena reports now count pre-resolved Image XObject color-space payloads, `PageResources` preserves indirect `/Properties` refs, Form resource overlays carry those refs, and the retained exact-tile dependency walker records active `/OC` marked-content property refs for tile-affecting paints. The same walker also records named color-space resources for Image XObject streams, referenced image `/Mask` and `/SMask` streams, retained inline images, native shading dictionaries, pattern resources, ExtGState soft-mask group color spaces, and direct color-space arrays that contain nested named color-space resources, including their transitive references. A changed OCG/OCMD property object or tint-transform/function behind an image, image-mask, inline-image, shading, pattern, SMask group, or nested `/Indexed`/tint-space base color-space resource can therefore invalidate only the recorded tile when exact tile coverage exists instead of relying solely on broader page/resource invalidation. **Latest annotation/widget AP dirty-route continuation:** cache-plan envelope application now normalizes nested AP-state dirty routes. `render/transaction_invalidation.rs` accepts explicit and mutation-prefixed AP ref fields, annotation/widget/form/signature AP refs, AP stream refs, normal/rollover/down appearance refs, appearance-state refs, and before/after appearance ref reports with structured object/generation keys; known refs map to source IDs and source cache markers, and unknown AP refs conservatively reset. **Latest renderer graph write-set alias continuation:** render-invalidation write-set normalization now accepts retained operation, display operation, display-list, render-resource, sublist, spatial-entry/index, backend-plan, cache-entry, and render-tile refs through snake_case, binding-style camelCase, and known mutation-prefixed fields. Known refs map through canonical source IDs/source-cache markers, while unknown refs keep the conservative reset path. **Latest packed image XObject color-space payload continuation:** packed Image XObject descriptors now pre-resolve first-level named image color-space resources from image stream dictionaries during resource-aware compilation. `ResolvedXObjectHandle.image_color_space` carries the resolved object into packed replay metadata, and the packed adapter validates the payload against the live image dictionary before decode. This removes another retained image replay path that could otherwise consult the page resource map by name at paint time. Evidence: `resource_aware_plan_pre_resolves_high_level_handles`, `packed_plan_replay_refuses_unresolved_resource_descriptors`, fmt check, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest managed render-cache handle smoke continuation:** .NET and Java now have focused local proof for the non-progressive caller-owned render-cache source surface. The .NET `RasterRenderingUsesTheCanonicalNativeSurface` smoke passed with the configured `.work` C ABI DLL on `PATH`, covering `RenderCache`, cached contract PNG/report rendering, render-invalidation-plan application, and cache clearing. Java main/test sources compiled directly with Java 25 preview enabled and include the matching `WellfriendPdf.RenderCache` smoke source. Source cache-handle adoption is no longer the blocker; external binding runtime/package matrix validation remains deferred. Evidence: focused `dotnet test` exit 0 and direct `javac --enable-preview --release 25` exit 0.
**Latest SDK feature-report wording continuation:** `sdk.rs::feature_report_json` now reports source-active typed-limit renderer categories and deferred external-validation categories instead of stale slash-later buckets. Progressive viewer queue/callback and cancellation entries identify source-exposed C/Python/WASM/.NET/Java/server surfaces while leaving external runtime matrix validation deferred. Evidence: `font_signature_decode_dedup_feature_envelopes`, fmt check, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest image capability unavailable-wording continuation:** runtime image-decode capability reporting now describes codec-native ROI, codestream-tile, and native progressive pixel output as unavailable for codec adapters that do not expose native partial support. This preserves accurate capability reporting for unsupported decoder APIs without claiming fake support or leaving a source TODO. Evidence: `renderer_capabilities_disclose_fallback_and_progressive_limits`.
**Latest runtime deferred-validation wording continuation:** runtime capabilities now describe external binding/runtime matrix work as validation deferred outside this prompt when the source/API surfaces already exist. Caller-owned surfaces, progressive viewer queue/callback helpers, render-cache telemetry, and renderer thread/cache matrix entries no longer present external matrix proof as a source implementation blocker. Evidence: `renderer_capabilities_disclose_fallback_and_progressive_limits` and server capability-route assertions.
**Latest Type 3 capability continuation:** `runtime.rs::runtime_capabilities_for` now reports retained Type 3 CharProc sublists as active source architecture instead of stale incomplete status. The capability reason names contract/resource/source-keyed immutable retained plans, cached retained-plan refusals, source-marker pruning, and typed ordinary-font/Unicode-alias refusal, while unsupported CharProc content remains typed refusal. Evidence: `renderer_capabilities_disclose_fallback_and_progressive_limits`, `type3_retained_charproc`, and `type3_program_cache`.
**Latest runtime image-decode capability continuation:** `runtime.rs::runtime_capabilities_for` now discloses the bounded progressive image decode lifecycle added in `render/image_decode_planning.rs`. The public capability reason records that native source-window, native reduction, or `requires_component_decode` work completes as `planned_partial_decode_complete`, while codecs without native partial/progressive pixel APIs remain explicit unavailable capabilities instead of being advertised as supported. Evidence: `renderer_capabilities_disclose_fallback_and_progressive_limits`.
**Latest alpha-mask fused clip-window allocation continuation:** `render/buffer.rs::AlphaMask::fused_clip_window` now reuses one bounded clip-opacity row scratch buffer across scanlines instead of allocating a fresh row vector for each destination row. The helper still fills the active clip opacity per row, dispatches to `wellfriendpdf_render_simd::multiply_alpha_rows`, and falls back to the existing scalar alpha product. This narrows the clip + soft-mask/image-mask fusion allocation boundary without claiming complete concrete-clip elimination. Evidence: `alpha_mask_fused_clip_window`, engine check, engine clippy, and fmt.
**Latest packed viewport hot-plan continuation:** `render/plan.rs::PackedDisplayList` now stores the compiled viewport and source byte estimate directly. `RenderSpatialIndex::compile`, `RenderPlan::execute_vector_tile_with_scratch`, and packed-plan byte accounting no longer dereference the retained source display list just to recover viewport dimensions or source size, so the vector tile hot path consumes packed plan state for tile-window construction and the memory estimator consumes cached compile-time accounting. The remaining transparent-page-group source-list dependency is closed by the packed metadata continuation below. Evidence: `packed_plan_uses_packed_viewport_for_vector_tile_execution`, `packed_vector_plan_replays_without_raw_content_cold_table`, engine check, engine clippy, and fmt.
**Latest retained transparent-page-group stat continuation:** `render/display_list.rs::DisplayListStats` now carries `requires_transparent_page_group`, derived during display-list classification from ExtGState alpha/blend/SMask resource dictionaries and Form XObject `/Group /S /Transparency` metadata in `PageResources`. Packed page rendering uses this retained stat for transparent-page-group planning instead of re-fetching raw page content and rescanning `ContentOperation` values after a display list is already compiled. Complete no-paint alpha ExtGStates remain non-page-group decisions. Evidence: `alpha_ext_gstate_does_not_force_page_compatibility_run`, `complete_no_paint_alpha_extgstate_does_not_require_page_group`, `transparent_form_xobject_sets_page_group_stat_without_raw_rescan`, engine check, engine clippy, and fmt.
**Latest packed transparent-page-group metadata continuation:** `render/plan.rs::PackedDisplayList` now captures `requires_transparent_page_group` during `compile_with_resources` and exposes it through `requires_transparent_page_group()`. The packed plan no longer stores `Arc<DisplayList>` or a `source()` accessor, and `render/page_renderer.rs` consumes the packed metadata for both uncached and cached packed replay. Evidence: `packed_plan_carries_transparent_page_group_stat_without_source_display_list`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest progressive image bounded-plan lifecycle continuation:** `render/image_decode_planning.rs::ProgressiveImageDecodeSession` now completes planned native source-window, reduced-resolution, or component-subset decode work with `planned_partial_decode_complete` instead of keeping those work items in a full-output continuation state. Plan-derived requests carry `requires_component_decode`, while default public constructor requests stay conservative. JPEG/JPX/JBIG2/lossless paths whose selected decoder APIs still lack native progressive or partial pixel continuation continue to report typed `full_decode_required` outcomes rather than fake support. Evidence: `progressive_native_window_plan_completes_without_full_decode_requirement`, `progressive_native_reduction_plan_completes_without_full_decode_requirement`, `progressive_native_component_subset_plan_completes_without_full_decode_requirement`, `progressive_image_decode_session_reports_full_decode_required`, `image_decode_planning`, engine check, engine clippy, and fmt.
**Latest packed color-space optimizer continuation:** packed state-run optimization now folds non-adjacent `CS`/`cs` color-space setters across independent no-paint state operations while preserving dependencies on `SC/SCN`, `sc/scn`, and device color setters. The slot tracker invalidates color-space/color candidates that would be reordered semantically, so safe no-paint color-space overwrites reduce hot descriptors without changing final paint state. Evidence: `packed_plan_overwrites_non_adjacent_color_space_across_independent_state`, `packed_plan_does_not_overwrite_color_space_across_generic_color_setter`, `packed_plan_does_not_overwrite_color_space_across_device_color_setter`, `packed_plan_`, engine check, engine clippy, and fmt.
**Latest packed matrix-composition optimizer continuation:** packed state-run optimization now composes adjacent and no-paint non-adjacent `cm` matrix concatenations before paint. `PackedDisplayList` detects consecutive `GraphicsStateDescriptor::ConcatMatrix` descriptors, replaces the previous hot descriptor with `concat_matrix(current, previous)`, tracks a CTM slot across independent state setters, and drops adjacent or separated inverse pairs whose composition is identity. This narrows RV-10 transform folding without crossing paint, scope, save/restore, clip, resource, ExtGState side-effect, or geometry boundaries. Evidence: `packed_plan_folds_adjacent_matrix_concatenation`, `packed_plan_folds_non_adjacent_matrix_concatenation_across_independent_state`, `packed_plan_folds_non_adjacent_inverse_matrix_concatenation_to_noop`, `packed_plan_`, fmt check, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest backend-plan resource-arena report continuation:** backend-plan arena reports now expose resource-specific packed payload counts beside descriptor-kind counts. `BackendPlanArenaReport.resource_arena_entries` and `BackendDocumentPlanArenaReport.total_resource_arena_entries` are derived from typed `NativeDescriptor` payloads and cover font, image, Form, Type 3, pattern, shading, transparency group, descriptor-level appearance placeholder, color-space including pre-resolved Image XObject color-space payloads, ExtGState, Properties, and inline-image arenas; annotation/widget appearance program cache telemetry remains separate in `RenderDocumentCache`. Evidence: `backend_plan_arena_report_exposes_resource_specific_payload_counts`, `backend_plan_arena_counts_image_xobject_color_space_payloads`, `backend_plan_arena`, fmt check, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest display-list active clip-DAG replay continuation:** `render/display_list.rs::CpuRenderDevice` now keeps an active `Arc<ClipNode>` in sync with the raster buffer. Display-list `q` pushes the active node directly, `W` intersects rectangle/path clip nodes through `ClipDag`, and `Q` reinstalls the saved node through a single helper instead of reclassifying the current materialized `ClipMask` at save time. Evidence: `cpu_render_device_save_restore_reuses_active_clip_dag_node`, `display_list`, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest active RenderState clip-DAG replay continuation:** `render/page_renderer.rs::RenderState` now owns `current_clip: Arc<ClipNode>` and uses it for page-content, retained display-list, packed-plan, Form cleanup, annotation appearance, tiling/shading pattern, Type 3 CharProc, SMask group, and transparency/Form group replay scopes. `apply_clip_node` composes against the active node, `install_clip_node` is the active-state buffer-sync boundary, and contract identity refresh re-interns the current clip into the new scoped DAG. Evidence: `q_restore_restores_previous_clip_mask`, `content_path_clip_replay_reuses_transformed_clip_node_cache`, `clip_dag`, `display_list`, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest transparency reusable backdrop-surface continuation:** bounded Form transparency groups now reuse offscreen-pool surfaces for backdrop windows instead of cloning fresh `PixelBuffer`s. `PixelBuffer::copy_rect_into_buffer` copies a source window into an existing destination surface without replacing clip/SMask/knockout metadata, `PixelBuffer::take_knockout_backdrop` returns stored backdrops for recycling, and `RenderState::handle_do_form_group` applies those helpers to non-isolated group backdrops, copied non-isolated group buffers, and knockout backdrop seeds. Evidence: `copy_rect_into_buffer_reuses_existing_surface`, `knockout_backdrop_can_be_extracted_for_reuse`, `transparency_group`, `knockout`, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest packed Form metadata continuation:** resource-aware packed Form XObject replay now carries the pre-resolved Form BBox and Matrix from `ResolvedXObjectHandle` into `RenderState::cached_form_xobject_program`. The active packed dispatcher constructs replay metadata from the compiled handle, so supported Form programs use compiled resource metadata instead of re-reading those dictionary fields at replay; legacy/non-resource-aware callers still fall back to dictionary extraction. Evidence: `resource_aware_plan_pre_resolves_high_level_handles`, `packed_plan_replay_refuses_unresolved_resource_descriptors`, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest transaction transitive source-dependency continuation:** `RenderDependencyGraph` now records source-to-source dependency edges and expands changed sources through dependents before invalidating pages/tiles. `RenderDocumentCache` uses the expanded set for source-scoped artifact pruning, so edits to nested shared resources evict both child cache entries and parent Form/pattern/annotation/Type 3 program caches keyed by the dependent source marker. The retained tile resource walker records parent-to-child source edges while walking bounded dependencies for resource-backed Forms, images, shadings, patterns, fonts, color spaces, and ExtGState dictionaries. Evidence: `changed_shared_resource_invalidates_dependent_source_tiles`, `changed_shared_resource_expands_transitive_dependents`, `shared_resource_invalidation_prunes_dependent_source_artifact_caches`, `bounded_form_xobject_records_transitive_resource_tile_dependency`, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest dirty-region cache-plan tile merge continuation:** Rust/server cache owners can now merge dirty-region rectangles into `RenderInvalidationCachePlan` exact tiles before applying the plan. `RenderInvalidationCachePlan::merge_dirty_region_tiles` reuses caller-supplied viewport/grid conversion, deduplicates tile entries, and lets `apply_to_cache` use exact-tile invalidation so page artifacts are pruned without expanding raster invalidation to every recorded page tile. Evidence: `render_invalidation_plan_merges_dirty_region_tiles_before_apply`, `transaction_invalidation`, engine check, fmt, and engine clippy.
**Latest C/Python/WASM render-contract builder parity continuation:** C ABI/header now expose `wellfriendpdf_render_contract_with_schema_policies`, a nullable-string schema-v1 builder for page box, execution mode, backend, compositing, annotations, forms, optional-content identity, text/image/path/subpixel smoothing, color scheme, print profile, halftone, overprint, rendering intent, color-management policy, exactness, and determinism. Python and WASM/TypeScript now expose method-level builders for the same policy fields. All three paths validate through the canonical Rust `RenderContract::validate`, and enum inputs accept canonical schema names plus common lowercase kebab/snake forms. Evidence: `capi_render_contract_schema_policy_builder_round_trips`, C/Python/WASM cargo checks, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
**Latest adaptive progressive scheduler policy continuation:** adaptive progressive jobs now emit a `ProgressiveTileSchedulerReport` in tokens and step reports. The selector keeps the supported fixed set `128/192/256/384/512`, starts from page area and temporary-memory budget, then deterministically reduces the target for render-plan and contract pressure: high-quality mode, print/proof profile, scalar backend, transparency ops, image density, clip pressure, and plan complexity tiers derived from display-list and packed-plan counts. Fixed tile requests remain fixed but are reported with the same schema. Tile scheduling now gives the tile containing the viewport center a distinct first priority before other visible tiles, matching the viewer queue's `center_visible_tile` class instead of relying only on visible-tile distance. Existing progressive JSON surfaces in Rust, server, C/Python/WASM/.NET/Java carry the report without independent binding schedulers. Evidence: `adaptive_tile_size_is_deterministic_and_budget_bounded`, `adaptive_scheduler_report_uses_contract_and_plan_complexity_pressure`, `zero_tile_dimension_selects_adaptive_size`, `center_visible_tile_orders_before_other_visible_tiles`, `step_report_includes_adjacent_page_prefetch_and_viewer_queue_preview`, server adaptive-start test, and C ABI check.
**Latest explicit contract backend-plan arena continuation:** render-view backend-plan arena reports now accept a full schema-v1 render contract instead of only page/dpi/mode defaults. The report includes arena kind, execution mode, backend selection, print profile, and output surface, and print/proof contracts now surface distinct CPU packed-plan arena identities through the Rust SDK, CLI `--contract-json`, server `contract_json`, C ABI/header, Python, WASM/TypeScript, .NET, and Java source shims. This is source-level plan observability only; GPU/debug backend-native arenas, print-native payloads, and external runtime binding matrices remain incomplete. Evidence: `render_view_exposes_print_contract_backend_plan_arena_report`, `backend_plan_arena_report_accepts_explicit_print_contract`, `backend_plan_arena_report`, CLI/server focused route tests, C/Python/WASM checks, .NET build, and direct Java 25 `javac` source checks.
**Latest nested retained active-resource stack continuation:** nested retained Form XObject, tiling-pattern tile, annotation-appearance, and Type 3 CharProc replay now scope the active pre-resolved resource side stack. If an inner packed plan records a fatal error after `q` and stops before `Q`, cleanup restores the caller's active-resource stack plus active font/color-space/pattern handles. Annotation appearances clear active pre-resolved resources while installing their fresh appearance graphics state, then restore the caller state. Type 3 retained CharProc replay now returns `false` when retained replay recorded a fatal error. Evidence: `active_resource_stack_after_inner_refusal` and `packed_plan_restore_restores_pre_resolved_active_font_resource`.
**Latest progressive image full-decode reporting continuation:** progressive image-decode lifecycle reports now mark `full_decode_required` from the actual plan shape: full source region at full resolution. Full raw/CCITT-style plans no longer suppress the flag merely because the codec family can window other clipped shapes, while reduced-resolution or source-windowed plans remain non-full-decode reports. Native progressive, JPX ROI, and codestream-tile support are still explicit unavailable capabilities. Evidence: `progressive_full_raw_plan_reports_full_decode_required_even_with_window_capability` and `progressive_image_decode_session_reports_full_decode_required`.
**Latest transaction shared-resource alias continuation:** render-invalidation nested write-set collection now recognizes form-field, form-value, AcroForm, optional-content state/config, Type 3 glyph/CharProc, font-descriptor, CMap, image-mask, soft-mask/image SMask, transparency group, widget/form/signature appearance, content stream, page content/program, page/page tree, catalog, metadata/XMP, Names/name tree/page labels, StructTree, ParentTree, RoleMap, page resource dictionary, render-relevant structure, output intent, ICC profile, transfer function, halftone, print profile, render-contract refs, image filter/decode/decode-params/interpolation/matte/color-key-mask refs, and JPEG/DCT/JPX/CCITT/JBIG2 parameter refs through explicit `changed_*_refs` aliases and structured object/generation keys. Known refs map into source cache markers; unknown refs still require conservative reset. Evidence: `nested_write_set_collector_accepts_shared_resource_aliases`, `nested_write_set_collector_accepts_render_structure_aliases`, `nested_write_set_collector_accepts_document_structure_and_group_aliases`, `nested_write_set_collector_accepts_image_decode_parameter_aliases`, and `transaction_invalidation`.
**Latest packed inline-image color-space continuation:** packed inline-image descriptors now carry a pre-resolved named color-space payload for well-formed `/ColorSpace /Name` and `/CS /Name` operands during resource-aware compilation. Missing named inline color-space resources fail typed at compile time, while built-in device spaces, inline abbreviations `/G`/`/RGB`/`/CMYK`, and image masks stay resource-object-free. Packed replay passes the resolved object to inline image painting instead of resolving the name through page resources. Evidence: `inline_image_descriptor`.
**Latest Type 3 retained subplan cache continuation:** full Type 3 CharProc replay now caches retained packed subplans as bounded document-owned `Type3ProgramCache<Type3RetainedCharProcPlan>` entries instead of compiling the retained display list and descriptors for each glyph render. Cache keys include parsed glyph identity, merged resources, the active retained render-contract fingerprint, and indirect CharProc source markers where available; cached refusal entries preserve the exact retained-plan failure reason. The cache participates in child-state absorption, budget eviction, source/page pruning, and resource-cache byte accounting. The follow-up source-identity slice also salts active parsed CharProc, retained subplan, geometry, device-mask, and coloured rendered-glyph cache entries with canonical document revision plus indirect `type3-program` markers where available, then prunes every matching Type 3 cache family during source-scoped invalidation. Evidence: `type3_retained_charproc`, `type3_program_cache`, `type3_charproc_render_cache_key`, `type3_source_pruning`, and `render_document_cache_budget_evicts_type3_parsed_program_caches`.
**Latest clip-DAG soft/image alpha window fusion continuation:** `ClipAlphaFusionWindow` and `ClipDag::fuse_alpha_mask_window` now expose a bounded-window DAG path for combining an existing clip node with alpha-bearing soft masks or image/stencil masks. The fused result is destination-local, uses the same rounded div-255 opacity multiplication as paint-time fusion, preserves the active clip DAG identity salts, simplifies binary/all-empty/all-full outputs to cheaper structural states, and short-circuits empty clips without full-page materialization. Evidence: `alpha_mask_window_fuses_with_clip_into_dense_dag_node`, `alpha_mask_window_empty_clip_short_circuits_to_empty_node`, and `binary_image_mask_window_simplifies_to_structural_clip_node`.
**Latest clip-DAG dimension-keyed materialization continuation:** `render/clip_dag.rs::ClipNode` now caches lazily materialized `ClipMask`s by destination dimensions rather than storing a single mask per node. Dimensionless structural states such as Full, Empty, and Rectangle can be shared and materialized at multiple output sizes without returning stale-size masks, while same-dimension calls still reuse the cached `Arc<ClipMask>`. `display_list.rs` restores saved clips by cloning from that dimension-keyed materialization result. Evidence: `materialize_cache_is_keyed_by_dimensions`, `clip_dag`, engine check, engine clippy, and workspace check.
**Latest PixelBuffer active-clip borrow continuation:** `PixelBuffer` hot paint-row paths now borrow the active `ClipMask` instead of cloning it for alpha-mask, cached RGBA, solid-fill, full-buffer composite, and offset-composite rows. `blend_alpha_mask_strided`, `blend_rgba_pixels_at`, `fill_rect`, `composite_from`, and `composite_from_at` split mutable pixel-row access from read-only clip-run inspection, so partial/binary/high-quality clip branches no longer allocate copied concrete masks before painting. This narrows the concrete-mask paint-boundary gap while leaving raster buffer clip installation and full clip/mask fusion incomplete. Evidence: `alpha_mask`, `fill_rect`, `composite_from`, `blend_rgba_pixels_at`, focused engine check, fmt, and clippy.
**Latest image XObject color-space/role identity continuation:** `render/page_renderer.rs::image_xobject_cache_key` now includes the resolved image color-space string plus image-mask and soft-mask role bits. Same-object XObject cache entries no longer collide when color-space family or mask semantics differ inside one render context; explicit color-space override objects continue to be hashed by the override key path. The helper's inline branch now also includes BPC, filters, color-space, mask roles, payload length, and payload hash instead of only page/size/length. Evidence: `image_xobject_cache_key_includes_color_space_and_mask_role`, `image_decode_cache_key`, engine check, engine clippy, and workspace check.

**Latest portable image decode cache identity continuation:** decoded image base keys now separate decoded-resource identity from pure placement. `render/page_renderer.rs::image_decode_cache_base_key` uses document revision, decoded source identity, and a decode-policy fingerprint for render mode, backend, print/exactness policy, CMM intent/backend, overprint, image smoothing, and resource budget, while dropping page number, viewport, tile origin, device transform, annotation/form policy, and optional-content state from the base key. Inline images use the same decode-policy identity while still hashing the complete inline payload. The final planner key still carries source region, target size, reduction, quality, component selection, target format, backend, decode array, DecodeParms, mask/SMask, interpolation, and decode-policy identity, so clipped or scaled placements remain distinct while compatible repeated decodes can be reused across pages, Forms, pattern cells, and viewer rerenders. Evidence: `image_decode_cache_key_uses_portable_decode_contract_identity`, `inline_image_decode_cache_key_hashes_full_payload_and_decode_policy`, `image_decode_cache_key`, `image_decode_planning`, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest standalone display-list replay fail-closed continuation:** `render/display_list.rs::render_display_list` now returns `Result<PixelBuffer>` and refuses unsupported or page-context-native display lists instead of logging warnings and returning pixels after skipping high-level native operations. The exported `replay_display_list` API now returns `Result<()>`, refuses unsupported lists, and requires a `RenderDevice::supports_native_high_level_ops` opt-in before dispatching native retained text, image, shading, pattern, inline-image, or Form XObject operations to device hooks. State/native hook methods no longer have default no-op or warning-only bodies, so replay devices must make state handling explicit and opt-in native devices must provide explicit handlers. Vector-only display lists still render through `CpuRenderDevice`; retained high-level operations must use `PageRenderer` display-list replay where resources and renderer state are available. Evidence: `standalone_display_list_replay_refuses_native_high_level_ops`, `direct_display_list_replay_refuses_native_high_level_ops_on_default_device`, `display_list`, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest transaction shared-resource alias continuation:** `render/transaction_invalidation.rs` now recognizes more nested shared-resource and document-structure write-set aliases from binding/server transaction envelopes, including XObject, ColorSpace, ExtGState/graphics-state, resource dictionary, tiling-pattern, appearance-stream, widget/form/signature appearance, Mask/SMask/image SMask, transparency group, OCG/OCMD/OC config, Properties, page/page tree, catalog, metadata/XMP, Names/name tree/page labels, StructTree, ParentTree, RoleMap, shared-resource, resource-write-set, and transitive write-set fields plus structured per-resource object/generation keys. Known refs merge into mapped source IDs and source-cache markers; unknown refs still trigger conservative reset. Evidence: `nested_write_set_collector_accepts_document_structure_and_group_aliases`, `transaction_invalidation`, engine check, and engine clippy.
**Latest registered and deterministic font-provider continuation:** `RegisteredFontProvider` and `ContentEngine::register_font_bytes` / `with_registered_font_bytes` now provide a caller-registered Rust runtime tier ahead of bundled fallback. Registered faces normalize subset prefixes and compacted names, report `FontProviderSource::UserRegistered`, `reason: DocumentProvided`, `resolution_source: "user_registered"`, and provider selection reason `user_registered_direct_match`, and salt the font policy identity plus font-byte cache keys with `registered-fonts:*` provider fingerprints. C ABI/header, Python, WASM/TypeScript, .NET, Java, CLI `--register-font NAME=PATH`, server render-contract upload fields, and progressive start upload fields now expose source registration paths; external runtime matrices remain incomplete. `BundledFontProvider` also implements the deterministic system-mapping tier before generic bundled fallback for stable host-family aliases: Arial/Calibri/Segoe UI/Tahoma/Verdana/Helvetica Neue map to Standard14 Helvetica-compatible bundled faces, Times New Roman/Georgia/Cambria/Constantia/Garamond map to Times-compatible faces, and Courier New/Consolas/Inconsolata/Lucida Console map to Courier-compatible faces. These selections report `FontProviderSource::DeterministicSystemMapping`, `reason: DeterministicSystemMapping`, and `resolution_source: "deterministic_system_mapping"` while symbolic names and Symbolic descriptors stay on the coverage fallback path instead of being mislabeled as sans mappings. High-quality rendering now accepts non-embedded deterministic aliases such as `ArialMT` and caller-registered fonts such as `SomeCorporateSans`, while the same generic font still refuses as generic bundled fallback when it is not registered. Evidence: `registered_provider_matches_subset_names_and_fingerprints_bytes`, `high_quality_render_accepts_caller_registered_font_mapping`, `document_font_cache_key_includes_contract_budget_and_font_provider_identity`, C/Python/WASM/CLI/server cargo checks, .NET build, Java source compile, `deterministic_system_aliases_report_mapping_source`, `symbolic_system_aliases_do_not_report_system_mapping`, `font_replacement_selection_labels_external_provider_sources`, `high_quality_render_accepts_deterministic_system_font_mapping`, `high_quality_render_refuses_generic_bundled_font_substitution`, and `symbolic_descriptor_routes_generic_compat_fallback_to_coverage_face`.
**Latest runtime font capability and dense clip materialization continuation:** `runtime.rs` now exposes `font_resolution_and_substitution_reporting` in runtime capability JSON, naming the caller-registered provider tier, deterministic-system source class, registered-provider fingerprint in font policy/cache identity, high-quality/exact acceptance of registered or deterministic-system faces, generic bundled refusal, and cross-binding source surfaces. `render/clip_dag.rs` now preserves overlapping dense partial-coverage alpha when a dense clip state is materialized at a different output size, clipping pixels outside the stored source extent instead of returning an empty mask. Evidence: `renderer_capabilities_disclose_fallback_and_progressive_limits` and `materialize_dense_alpha_size_mismatch_preserves_overlap`.
**Latest scaled image decode reservation and marker-audit continuation:** native scaled JPEG and JPX Image XObject scheduling now reserves decode memory from the requested reduced target dimensions instead of maxing the reservation against the full uncompressed source image. This makes target-resolution decode usable under target-sized resource budgets while the decoder still enforces stream and output limits. Unsupported packed state descriptors now carry only the operator required for typed compile refusal, not raw operand payloads. Stale display-list compatibility-run counters were removed because compatibility sections are typed state descriptors/refusals rather than fallback runs; tile-stitch coverage now asserts one shared display-list entry and four tile-specific transparent-page-group entries. The source marker audit for renderer/image/font/SIMD/runtime code also returns no prompt marker hits after ExtGState `/SA`, `/AIS`, and `/TK` visible-paint failures were clarified as typed exact-policy refusals rather than implementation stubs. Evidence: `scaled_jpeg_xobject_scheduler_reserves_target_size_not_full_source`, `scaled_jpx_xobject_scheduler_reserves_target_size_not_full_source`, `unsupported_state`, `compile_refusal`, `graphics_state_descriptor_unknown_operator_is_unsupported`, `display_list_tile_stitch_matches_full_page`, `extgstate_`, and the scoped marker `rg`.
**Latest print separation policy contract continuation:** `render/print_profile.rs::validate_print_profile_prepress` now refuses `OverprintPolicy::PreserveSeparations` for the active render-contract target even when `NativeLittleCms` is requested, because the renderer publishes bounded RGB/gray raster output and exposes Separation/DeviceN plate data through the prepress plate report rather than n-channel output surfaces. `OverprintPolicy::Preview` and RGB ordered halftone remain supported raster policies; proof annotation visibility is asserted at the print-profile flag boundary instead of through an invalid non-proofed raster shortcut. Evidence: `preserve_separations`, `print_profile`, and `overprint_preview_policy_is_accepted_by_contract_renderer`.
**Latest clip-DAG dense alpha fusion continuation:** `render/clip_dag.rs::ClipState::intersect_structural` now fuses dense alpha clips with rectangles, binary sparse/RLE row masks, and other dense alpha clips without creating `Composite` materialization nodes. Dense/dense intersections use bytewise minimum coverage, dense/binary intersections zero outside visible runs, and the alpha-byte classifier simplifies all-empty, all-full, or binary results back to cheaper structural states before retaining a dense mask. The stale exact-image fixture was corrected to use a real DCT/JPEG filtered image for unavailable source-region decode refusal. Evidence: `dense_binary_intersection_fuses_dense_without_materialization`, `dense_rectangle_intersection_fuses_dense_without_materialization`, `dense_dense_intersection_fuses_min_alpha_without_materialization`, and `exact_contract_refuses_visible_image_requiring_unavailable_region_decode`.
**Latest ResearchHybrid image decode target-format continuation:** `ImageDecodeTargetFormat::for_backend` now maps the active CPU `ResearchHybrid` backend to the current raw 8-bit interleaved decoded-image target, matching `StandardCpu` and `ScalarReference`, while `ImageDecodeBackendIdentity` continues to reserve a separate `research-hybrid` cache-key fragment. The stale unimplemented premultiplied RGBA target enum value was removed, so active cache keys name only decoded target shapes the renderer actually produces. Evidence: `cache_key_includes_target_format_and_backend_identity`.
**Latest render-contract research execution-mode continuation:** active CPU render contracts now accept `ExecutionMode::Research` as an identity-bearing field. The local renderer keeps deterministic CPU execution, preserves distinct contract/cache identity, and runtime capability reporting names the active research execution-mode boundary; research-only GPU/distributed/solver/autotuning capabilities remain policy-gated separately. Evidence: `contract_research_execution_mode_renders_with_distinct_identity`, `contract_reports_unsupported_semantic_fields_by_name`, and runtime capability disclosure checks.
**Latest render-contract research-hybrid backend continuation:** active CPU render contracts now accept `BackendSelection::ResearchHybrid` as a guarded CPU hybrid-dispatch backend. The backend uses the active CPU SIMD/portable/scalar dispatcher instead of the scalar-only guard, preserves separate render-contract and image-decode cache identity, and no longer appears in unsupported-field diagnostics. The server render-contract builder accepts the public `research-hybrid` token and returns normalized `ResearchHybrid` contract JSON. GPU/printer/debug backend arenas and fully backend-native packed payloads remain incomplete. Evidence: `contract_research_hybrid_backend_renders_with_distinct_identity`, `contract_reports_unsupported_semantic_fields_by_name`, `render_contract_builder_accepts_research_hybrid_backend`, and runtime capability disclosure checks.
**Latest prepress plate report continuation:** `ContentEngine::prepress_plate_report` is now exposed through `sdk::prepress_plate_report_json`, CLI `prepress-plate-report`, server `POST /api/v1/prepress/plate-report`, C ABI/header, Python, WASM/TypeScript, .NET, and Java source shims. The report surfaces deterministic Separation/DeviceN plate order, plate summaries, contribution counts, bounded memory accounting, and plate cache fingerprints for supported fill/stroke paths without claiming full separation-preserving print output or native CMM runtime validation.
**Latest local continuation:** calibrated image ColorSpace parameter refusal for direct CalGray/CalRGB/Lab images and Indexed calibrated base palettes; active/direct shading metadata refusal for missing `ShadingType`, non-mesh direct mesh types, missing Type 5 row metadata, and direct shading helper metadata defaults; SMask transfer-function shape refusal; active pattern `/PatternType` required-metadata refusal; named-color tint-transform shape refusal with a DeviceN-specific guard against single-input transform shapes; SVG-in-OpenType geometry parsing now preserves omitted optional SVG defaults while refusing present malformed numeric geometry attributes and negative `stroke-width`; active Type 3 glyph CTM construction refuses present malformed `/FontMatrix` values; Type 3 rendered-glyph cache bounds now require exact four-number finite `/FontBBox` arrays instead of truncating overlong font bounds, with malformed font-level bounds declining cached bounded surfaces and falling back to full CharProc rendering; active inline image, Image XObject, and referenced explicit-mask `/Filter` metadata now requires a name or all-name array before bpc/color-space planning instead of dropping malformed array entries and applying terminal-codec defaults; active non-mask image `/Decode` arrays now require exact `2 * channel_count` finite numeric values instead of filtering nonnumeric entries, ignoring extras, or decoding from a truncated mapping; active color-key image `/Mask` arrays now require exact finite integer ranges and fail typed instead of filtering bad entries, ignoring extras, clamping, rounding, or swapping reversed ranges; active Form XObject, annotation appearance, SMask `/G`, and SVG/PS vector Form paths now require exact four-number finite `/BBox` metadata instead of replaying unclipped or with dropped local Form bounds; visible annotations now require exact finite `/Rect` metadata before appearance selection or synthesis instead of being silently skipped; synthesized text-markup, line, and ink annotation geometry is preflighted before building appearances instead of full-Rect approximations, skipped strokes, or filtered coordinates; synthesized annotation `/C` and `/CA` values now reject present malformed or out-of-range paint metadata instead of defaulting colors or clamping opacity; synthesized FreeText `/Contents` now rejects malformed present display metadata instead of filtering arrays before text layout; synthesized widget `/MK /BG` and `/MK /BC` colors now reject malformed or out-of-range values before widget appearance construction instead of defaulting or clamping chrome colors; synthesized widget `/DA` now rejects malformed present `Tf`, `g`, `rg`, and `k` font/color operands instead of silently using default text style or clamped color; synthesized widget `/V`, `/Opt`, and `/MK /CA` display metadata now rejects malformed present values instead of filtering arrays, skipping invalid options, or dropping captions; active optional-content visibility now rejects malformed OCG/OCMD configuration, missing property resources, cyclic references, and unsupported OCMD policies instead of treating them as visible; active ExtGState render-state metadata now rejects malformed or unsupported present alpha, line style, miter, blend, rendering intent, overprint, stroke adjustment, alpha source, text knockout, transfer function, flatness, smoothness, dash, and font values before display-list capture, immediate replay, or packed graphics-state mutation instead of retaining defaults, clamping values, or filtering malformed arrays; SVG/PS regional image classification and replay now reject malformed present `/ImageMask`/`/IM` or `/Interpolate`/`/I` booleans before bounded regional output instead of defaulting them to `false`; lower-level image normalization and CCITT DecodeParms parsing now reject name-valued boolean spellings before stencil-mask channel selection or CCITT decode.
**Latest EditingTransactions/TextReflow routing continuation:** `editing_transactions.rs` no longer blanket-refuses `geometric_block` and `semantic_document` scene text transactions before the canonical TextReflow subsystem can handle them. Geometric requests route through `text_reflow::apply_reflow_region`, semantic requests route through `text_reflow::apply_reflow_document`, and the adapter converts TextReflow source instructions, object/stream refs, affected pages, typed refusals, inverse metadata, and dirty regions back into the existing EditingTransactions render-invalidation report shape. The compact scene text request now forwards explicit TextReflow region, allowed-expansion, next-region/column, downstream vector/link movement, layout constraint, language, direction, alignment, line-height, hyphenation, page-creation, font-reduction, signature, and low-confidence review-approval fields while still mapping `preserve_original_or_refuse` to TextReflow's `preserve_original_per_run` source-style policy.
**Latest direct shading helper-local continuation:** `render/shading.rs` now makes the direct Type 1/2/3 and mesh paint helpers use strict finite array, boolean, color-space, and transform boundaries locally. Present malformed `/Coords`, `/Domain`, `/Extend`, Type 1 `/Matrix`, malformed shading `/ColorSpace` metadata, unsupported named color-space resolution, singular/non-finite paint CTMs, or singular Type 1 shading matrices return without paint even if a future caller reaches those helpers below `validate_shading_dictionary_for_paint`; absent optional `/Domain`, `/Extend`, and Type 1 `/Matrix` still use the PDF defaults. Mesh vertex and patch readers now index exact validated `/Decode` metadata directly instead of retaining lower decode defaults.
**Latest ExtGState helper-local continuation:** `content/state.rs::GraphicsState::try_apply_ext_g_state` now validates ExtGState render metadata before graphics-state mutation, while well-formed `/SA`, `/AIS`, and `/TK` values are retained in graphics state instead of being rejected as metadata-only unsupported flags. Active page rendering, packed descriptor replay, display-list capture, SVG replay, PostScript replay, vector-output classification, and text collector state tracking use the strict helper before the legacy mutator can clamp out-of-range alpha/line values, filter malformed arrays, or partially apply malformed font/blend/dash state. Visible paint that observes unsupported stroke-adjustment, alpha-source, or disabled-text-knockout semantics now fails at active/retained/packed paint boundaries.
**Latest JPEG/JPX reduction exactness continuation:** `render/image_decode_planning.rs` now admits a native DCT/JPEG or JPX reduced-resolution tier only when the reduced decoder output covers both requested target axes. Asymmetric downscale requests that would undersample one axis keep `reduction_level = None` and remain on the exact full-resolution or typed unsupported boundary instead of entering cache identity as an unsafe reduced decode.
**Latest JPX metadata-inspection continuation:** `images/jpx.rs::inspect_metadata` now exposes JPX dimensions, original bit depth, color-space family, color/stored channel counts, and alpha state without decoding pixels. `ImageDecodeCapabilityReport` now serializes `metadata_inspection`, and document-level image capability JSON reports `native_metadata_inspection_count`; JPX metadata inspection is native while ROI/region, codestream tile, component-subset, and progressive pixel output remain explicit limitations. The raw unfiltered decoder now separately supports native selected-component source-window output for XObjects and inline images across 1/2/4/8/16-bit samples.
**Latest packed replay pre-resolved resource continuation:** `render/page_renderer.rs::RenderStatePlanAdapter` now requires packed Image XObject, Form XObject, named shading, ExtGState, font, non-device named color-space, named pattern, and `/OC` marked-content property descriptors to carry pre-resolved resource payloads during replay. Missing handles/objects/dictionaries record typed fatal replay refusals instead of falling back to runtime resource-name lookup through `handle_do`, `handle_sh`, page resource maps, or later font-resource resolution. Built-in DeviceGray/DeviceRGB/DeviceCMYK/Pattern color-space names remain valid without resource objects. `render/plan.rs::GraphicsStateDescriptor::to_content_operation` is documented as a descriptor round-trip/diagnostic helper only; active packed replay remains on `PlanDispatcher`.
**Latest packed culled text advancement continuation:** `render/page_renderer.rs::RenderStatePlanAdapter` now routes culled non-clipping `Tj`, `TJ`, `'`, and `"` descriptors through a no-paint `RenderState` text-advance path. The path decodes glyphs with the active font resolver, applies PDF/font/Type 3 advance metrics, honors character and word spacing, preserves `TJ` adjustments, and leaves clipping text modes on normal replay because they can change clip state. The old no-op advance helper and culled-`TJ` early return are removed. Evidence: `packed_culled_text_array_advances_text_state_without_painting` and `packed_culled_spacing_next_line_show_advances_text_state_without_painting`.
**Latest scan-converter convex telemetry continuation:** `render/path.rs::PathRasterStats` now publishes `convex_fast_pixels` from the existing convex sampled-span counter, and CLI render telemetry emits `convex_fast_pixels_delta` in both summary paths. The guarded convex fill fast path is now visible to source telemetry and future verification harnesses instead of only to private tests. Evidence: `convex_polygon_fill_fast_path_matches_general_scanline` and `concave_polygon_fill_rejects_convex_fast_path`.
**Latest active shading function-array continuation:** `render/function.rs` now evaluates a shading `/Function` array as a bounded list of one-output component functions. `render/shading.rs` uses the array-aware evaluator for Type 1, axial/radial, and mesh shading sampling, while `render/page_renderer.rs::validate_shading_dictionary_for_paint` accepts well-formed arrays and still refuses empty, oversized, malformed, or multi-output component arrays before paint. Evidence: `function_array`, `axial_shading_function_array_paints_component_gradient`, and `malformed_active_shading_dictionary_returns_typed_refusal`.
**Latest render-contract image-smoothing continuation:** active CPU render contracts now accept `SmoothingPolicy::Disabled`, `SmoothingPolicy::Antialiased`, and `SmoothingPolicy::Subpixel` for `image_smoothing`. `render/page_renderer.rs` threads the policy into the retained plan contract, active `RenderState`, annotation replay policy, and nested soft-mask/transparency-group states; inline images and Image XObjects validate `/Interpolate` first, then `Disabled` forces nearest-neighbour sampling while `Antialiased` and `Subpixel` preserve PDF interpolation. Evidence: `contract_image_smoothing_disabled_overrides_interpolate_true`, `contract_subpixel_text_uses_lcd_glyph_mask_policy`, and `contract_reports_unsupported_semantic_fields_by_name`.
**Latest render-contract text-smoothing continuation:** active CPU render contracts now accept `SmoothingPolicy::Disabled`, `SmoothingPolicy::Antialiased`, and `SmoothingPolicy::Subpixel` for `text_smoothing`, plus the separate `subpixel_text` policy. `render/page_renderer.rs` threads the policies into the retained plan contract, active `RenderState`, annotation replay policy, and nested soft-mask/transparency-group states; `GlyphMaskCacheKey` includes binary-alpha state; cached glyph masks are thresholded for disabled smoothing; direct ordinary/color glyph fill fallback paths use binary scanline coverage under disabled smoothing; and subpixel text paints cached or direct glyph masks through an RGB LCD-style per-channel compositor. Evidence: `contract_text_smoothing_disabled_thresholds_glyph_edges`, `contract_subpixel_text_uses_lcd_glyph_mask_policy`, and `contract_reports_unsupported_semantic_fields_by_name`.
**Latest render-contract path-smoothing continuation:** active CPU render contracts now accept `SmoothingPolicy::Disabled`, `SmoothingPolicy::Antialiased`, and `SmoothingPolicy::Subpixel` for `path_smoothing`. `render/page_renderer.rs` threads the policy into the retained plan contract, active `RenderState`, annotation replay policy, and nested soft-mask/transparency-group states; path fill/stroke mask cache keys include binary-alpha state; cached path masks are thresholded for disabled smoothing; direct RGB plus CMYK overprint-preview path fallbacks use binary scanline coverage under disabled smoothing; and `Subpixel` uses the existing device-space subpixel antialias coverage path. Evidence: `contract_path_smoothing_disabled_thresholds_fill_edges`, `contract_subpixel_text_uses_lcd_glyph_mask_policy`, and `contract_reports_unsupported_semantic_fields_by_name`.
**Latest render-contract scalar-reference backend continuation:** active CPU render contracts now accept `BackendSelection::ScalarReference`. `render/buffer.rs` provides a scoped scalar-compositor guard so contract rendering reports `scalar` and declines architecture SIMD plus portable-wide row helpers for that render, falling through to the existing scalar compositors. The later research-hybrid continuation makes `ResearchHybrid` active through the guarded CPU dispatcher rather than the scalar-only guard, and `render/page_renderer.rs` carries backend policy through retained plan contracts, active/child render states, annotation replay, SMask cache identity, and inline/Image XObject decode-plan/cache identity. Evidence: `contract_scalar_reference_backend_uses_scalar_compositor`, `contract_research_hybrid_backend_renders_with_distinct_identity`, and `contract_reports_unsupported_semantic_fields_by_name`.

**Latest native unpremultiply SIMD continuation:** native x86/x86_64 `render-simd` builds now dispatch `unpremultiply_rgba` through a guarded SSE2 four-pixel load/store row path when available. The reciprocal conversion remains scalar per channel to preserve the exact byte contract, with alpha-zero handling, scalar tails, and scalar debug-oracle checks. Evidence: `unpremultiply_public_uses_sse2_when_available`, `sse` filter, crate check, and crate clippy.

**Latest native mixed-destination alpha-mask SIMD continuation:** native x86/x86_64 `render-simd` builds now dispatch `blend_alpha_mask_normal` through a guarded SSE2 four-pixel load/store row path when available. The helper preserves the exact normal Compat mixed-destination source-over byte contract, with scalar tails and scalar debug-oracle checks. Evidence: `blend_alpha_mask_normal_public_uses_sse2_when_available`, `sse` filter, public-kernel scalar-equivalence test, crate check, and crate clippy.

**Latest native separable blend SIMD continuation:** native x86/x86_64 `render-simd` builds now dispatch the full `blend_separable_opaque_destination` PDF separable set through a guarded SSE2 row path when available. Multiply, Screen, Overlay, Darken, Lighten, HardLight, Difference, and Exclusion use exact integer lane math; ColorDodge, ColorBurn, and SoftLight use SSE float lanes with grouped gather/scatter and scalar-oracle checks. Evidence: `blend_separable_opaque_dst_public_uses_sse2_when_available`, `separable` and `sse` filters, public-kernel scalar-equivalence test, crate check, and crate clippy.

**Latest strict vector-output fallback continuation:** SVG and PostScript now expose strict vector-output entry points that refuse whole-page raster fallback with typed `UnsupportedFeature`: `render_page_svg_strict`, `render_page_ps_strict`, `ContentEngine::render_page_svg_strict`, `ContentEngine::render_page_ps_strict`, `render_document_ps_strict`, and `render_page_eps_strict`. The CLI `render` command exposes the exact/refusal surface through `--strict-vector` for SVG, PS, and EPS, includes `strict_vector` in JSON summaries, and rejects the flag for raster formats. Compatibility SVG/PS/EPS export retains explicit `is_rasterized` whole-page output for legacy conversion, but runtime `renderer_fallback_policies` now reports zero material-degrading high-quality rows and zero high-quality canonical-immediate routes. Evidence: `svg_strict_refuses_alpha_group_whole_page_raster_fallback`, `ps_strict_refuses_alpha_group_whole_page_raster_fallback`, `ps_strict_refuses_normal_alpha_extgstate_whole_page_raster_fallback`, `render_strict_vector_modes_run_and_report`, `render_strict_vector_rejects_raster_formats`, and `renderer_capabilities_disclose_fallback_and_progressive_limits`.
**Latest transparent-blend no-paint continuation:** PostScript vector classification now accepts supported alpha/blend ExtGState entries as state and moves the exactness decision to paint-time. A fill or stroke whose relevant alpha is zero remains a vector no-op even when the unused alpha channel is fractional or `/BM` is non-normal; visible fractional-alpha or non-normal-blend paint still fails closed to the typed whole-page raster/refusal boundary. Top-level transparent-page-group planning still uses the complete no-paint test before allocating a page backdrop. Evidence: `postscript_transparent_non_normal_blend_paint_is_stateful_vector_noop`, `postscript_stroke_noop_ignores_fractional_fill_alpha`, `visible_postscript_alpha_or_blend_paint_stays_whole_page`, `postscript_visible_non_normal_blend_or_alpha_remains_conservative`, `transparent_page_group_ignores_complete_no_paint_blend_state`, and `vector_fallback`.
**Latest opaque-background flatten SIMD continuation:** Compat page-background flattening now tries `render-simd::flatten_opaque_background` before the existing portable-wide fallback. Native x86/x86_64 SSE2 and wasm32+`simd128` row groups preserve the scalar byte source-over contract and force opaque alpha, with scalar debug oracles and scalar tails. Evidence: `flatten_opaque_background` render-simd test, public-kernel scalar-equivalence test, wasm32+`simd128` crate check, engine `flatten_compat_opaque_background` tests, runtime capability test, crate checks, and crate clippies.
**Latest active clip-DAG identity continuation:** `render/page_renderer.rs` now creates active page, SMask `/G`, and transparency/Form group `ClipDag` instances with `ClipIdentityScope` derived from document revision, active render-contract fingerprint, and tile-local viewport identity. Explicit contract policy application refreshes the DAG scope after replacing the legacy fingerprint, so newly interned clip nodes no longer retain the default zero identity scope.
**Latest transparency group color-space policy continuation:** `render/page_renderer.rs` now validates Form transparency-group and SMask `/G` `/Group /CS` values before group rendering. DeviceRGB/sRGB/default group spaces continue through the active RGB compositor; DeviceGray and DeviceCMYK active Form groups must prove the opaque-normal inert Form-stream subset before rendering, and alpha/backdrop-observable non-RGB active Form groups fail typed before offscreen allocation instead of silently using RGB group-space compositing. Opaque normal Form groups with well-formed CalGray, CalRGB, Lab, Indexed, or ICCBased `/CS` values render through a non-device-inert policy; `/S /Alpha` SMask `/G` groups with no `/BC` can also use that policy because only alpha is consumed. Luminosity SMask `/BC` now resolves well-formed CalGray, CalRGB, Lab, Indexed, ICCBased, Separation, and DeviceN group spaces through the named-color/CMM resolver, and cache identity includes the resolved non-device color-space fingerprint. Malformed arrays, missing resources, unresolved references, malformed Indexed palettes, malformed tint transforms, malformed ICCBased profile references or `/N` metadata, alpha SMask non-device groups with `/BC`, alpha-bearing non-device Forms, and richer non-device group spaces fail typed before offscreen allocation or SMask group execution.
**Latest SVG/PS named paint color continuation:** `render/svg.rs` and `render/postscript.rs` now recheck named paint color-space resolution inside the vector sinks. Valid `/Separation /None` no-paint remains transparent, but malformed, missing, invalid, or unsupported named paint color spaces become fatal vector-output errors instead of transparent paint or compatibility black fallback if a sink is reached below classifier preflight.
**Latest SVG blend-mode continuation:** `render/vector_fallback.rs` now makes ExtGState `/BM` safety target-aware. Conservative/no-reader paths still require first-supported Normal/Compatible blend semantics, SVG accepts supported PDF blend modes when the rest of the ExtGState is safe, and PostScript accepts supported alpha/blend state but refuses visible fractional-alpha or non-normal-blend paint at the operation boundary. `render/svg.rs` emits native `mix-blend-mode` styles on path, glyph, regional image, inline-image, shading, shading-pattern, and tiling-pattern paint output. PostScript visible fractional-alpha/non-normal-blend paint, soft masks, and semantic transparency groups remain on the conservative fallback boundary.
**Latest generic device color-component continuation:** `render/plan.rs`, `render/display_list.rs`, and `render/page_renderer.rs` now validate generic `SC`/`SCN`/`sc`/`scn` operands against the active device color-space arity before retained capture or active rendering mutates paint state. Malformed `/DeviceGray`, `/DeviceRGB`, and `/DeviceCMYK` generic component vectors now mark retained display lists unsupported or return typed render refusal instead of reaching padded/truncated `ColorSpaceHandler` conversion.
**Latest render-facing device color-state continuation:** `render/color.rs::ColorSpaceHandler::strict_to_render_color` now gives active page paint, retained display-list simple-color capture, Type 3 inherited vector color replay, and SVG/PostScript vector sinks a shared exact finite `/DeviceGray`, `/DeviceRGB`, and `/DeviceCMYK` graphics-state boundary below PDF operand validation. Malformed internal device color state now records typed render/vector-output refusal or declines retained metadata instead of padding missing components, truncating extras, or emitting unknown fallback pixels.
**Latest synthesized/Type3 DeviceCMYK continuation:** `render/page_renderer.rs` no longer keeps a local algebraic `(1-c)*(1-k)` CMYK conversion for synthesized annotation `/C`, widget `/MK` colors, widget `/DA k` text colors, or Type 3 device-color pixels. These paths now normalize finite unit components and route through `render/cmm.rs::device_cmyk_to_srgb`, matching image decode and overprint-preview conversion policy. Evidence: `synthesized_annotation_cmyk_uses_shared_device_cmyk_fallback`.
**Latest progressive image-decode lifecycle continuation:** `render/image_decode_planning.rs::ProgressiveImageDecodeSession::continue_decode` now treats `Completed`, `Cancelled`, `Failed`, and `Closed` as terminal before checking native-progressive capability, so future native progressive decoders cannot resurrect a cancelled, failed, session-closed, or document-closed decode session into `Completed`. `ProgressiveImageDecodeReport` carries a typed `release_reason`; `cancel`, `fail`, `close`, and `close_for_document_close` release retained state with cancellation, render-failure, session-close, or document-close reasons; terminal reports suppress `full_decode_required`; and the SDK JSON envelope covers continue-after-cancel, continue-after-fail, continue-after-close, and continue-after-document_close. The planner comments now reflect active CCITT/raw source-window and JPEG/JPX reduction paths while keeping progressive pixel continuation unavailable. Evidence: `progressive_image_decode_continue_preserves_terminal_states`, `progressive_image_decode_document_close_releases_state`, `progressive_image_decode_pause_resume_cancel_close_release_state`, `progressive_image_decode_lifecycle_report_envelope`, and `capi_progressive_image_decode_lifecycle_report_json`.
**Latest retained-list refusal continuation:** `render/page_renderer.rs` and CLI `render-corpus --pipeline display-list` now refuse unsupported retained display-list replay in compatibility mode instead of delegating to canonical raw/immediate dispatch. Full-page direct, cached, contract, display-list helper, direct display-list replay, cached display-list replay, retained tile, public tile helper, band, progressive, and render-corpus display-list page paths all propagate typed `UnsupportedFeature`; high-quality/exact paths keep the existing `HighQualityExact` refusal. Evidence: `high_quality_display_list_page_helpers_refuse_unsupported_list`, `high_quality_display_list_tile_refuses_unsupported_list`, `compat_display_list_bands_refuse_unsupported_list_without_immediate_fallback`, `compat_progressive_refuses_unsupported_display_list_tile_without_immediate_fallback`, `high_quality_progressive_refuses_unsupported_display_list_tile`, and `render_corpus_display_list_refuses_unsupported_retained_replay`.
**Latest marked-content/restore side-stack continuation:** `render/page_renderer.rs` no longer lets the lower-level optional-content visibility pop restore to visible when reached without an active marked-content sequence, and `render/display_list.rs` no longer lets a retained `Q` restore default clip bounds, color explicitness, or active soft-mask state when the graphics-state side stacks are desynchronized. Both paths now fail typed at the helper boundary.
**Latest retained text helper-local continuation:** `render/display_list.rs` now validates operands inside `RetainedTextOp::from_content_operation` before constructing retained text descriptors, preserving malformed text refusal reasons in the retained unsupported descriptor. `render/page_renderer.rs` now treats that descriptor as fatal during native retained text replay instead of skipping it, while the normal validated native text path remains replayable.
**Latest retained restore replay continuation:** `render/page_renderer.rs` now rejects retained display-list and packed-plan `Restore` underflow before graphics-state mutation. Direct retained replay and `RenderStatePlanAdapter::dispatch_restore` require matching graphics-state, clip, and SMask side-stack entries, so malformed retained plans cannot continue with stale clip or mask state after warning-only restore handling.
**Latest page-content/Form restore side-stack continuation:** `render/page_renderer.rs` now applies the same pre-mutation side-stack principle to active page-content `Q` replay and Form XObject cleanup. Page-content `Q` refuses missing clip/SMask side-stack entries before popping graphics state, and Form cleanup records a typed fatal refusal when its clip/SMask sentinels are unavailable instead of warning and continuing with stale or default clip/mask state.
**Latest color-glyph paint continuation:** SVG-in-OpenType static color-glyph paint parsing now rejects out-of-range `rgb()` numeric components, percentage components, and opacity attributes instead of clamping them into visible paint; omitted optional paint metadata still uses the SVG defaults.
**Latest font-substitution continuation:** FontDescriptor Symbolic/Nonsymbolic, Italic, ForceBold, and FontWeight hints now feed deterministic replacement selection. Unknown non-Standard14 symbolic descriptor fonts route through the symbolic coverage lookup and report `CoverageOnly` bundled fallback identity in compatibility mode, while high-quality/exact rendering still refuses generic bundled replacement.
**Latest direct PDFium harness continuation:** the standalone C harness now draws supported AcroForm/widget appearances through PDFium form-fill (`FPDFDOC_InitFormFillEnvironment`, page form lifecycle calls, and `FPDF_FFLDraw`), reports typed `forms_policy` errors for form rendering combined with matrix, clip, or crop-box controls that cannot be represented by the PDFium form-fill viewport API, records effective matrix/clip plus raw byte metadata in JSONL, discloses serial worker execution, emits a stable JSONL `failure_class` beside every error code/detail, and passes a compile-only MSVC `/W4 /WX` source guard against stub public PDFium headers without provisioning PDFium.
**Latest SVG/PS regional order/bounds continuation:** regional Image XObject and inline-image SVG/PostScript emission now carries inert kind and device-space bounds metadata, and focused Image XObject plus inline-image tests assert each raster segment remains ordered between surrounding native vector paths. This is source-only order/bounds evidence, not full RB-14 closure.
**Latest SVG/PS linear shading-domain continuation:** simple Type 2 vector shading regionalization now accepts finite, non-degenerate non-unit shading `/Domain` intervals, including intervals that cross an `N=1` Type 2 function domain. SVG emits explicit native stops at function-domain boundaries, and PostScript emits a Type 3 stitching function when more than two stops are required; malformed and non-linear cases stay on the conservative fallback path.
**Latest SVG/PS shading BBox continuation:** accepted simple vector shadings now carry non-degenerate `/BBox` metadata into SVG/PostScript replay. Direct native gradients and shading-pattern replay are clipped to the transformed shading BBox; malformed or degenerate BBoxes remain conservative fallback cases.
**Latest SVG/PS Type 2 function-array shading continuation:** simple vector shading regionalization now accepts `/Function` arrays when each element resolves to a linear Type 2 component function and the concatenated endpoint outputs form a supported color vector. Malformed arrays, empty arrays, non-linear elements, oversized outputs, and malformed or degenerate domains remain conservative fallback cases.
**Latest SVG/PS Type 3 stitching-function shading continuation:** simple vector shading regionalization accepts continuous Type 3 stitching functions whose segments are linear Type 2 subfunctions with consistent component counts and valid bounds/encode metadata. SVG emits flattened native stops; PostScript emits native `shfill` with a LanguageLevel 3 stitching function. A later PostScript-specific exact-function path also admits direct DeviceRGB/DeviceGray discontinuous Type 3 stitching when its Type 2 segments can be serialized exactly as RGB; SVG keeps discontinuous stitching conservative to avoid inexact gradients.
**Latest SVG/PS ICCBased Gray/RGB/native-CMYK shading-color continuation:** simple vector shading regionalization now accepts resource-named Gray and RGB `/ICCBased` axial and radial shadings in the default qcms path when the profile declares `/N 1` or `/N 3` and sampled Type 2 function components exactly match that profile channel count. Four-channel `/N 4` ICCBased vector shadings are admitted only when `native-cmm-lcms2` is active, routing through native LittleCMS; other ICCBased vector shapes remain conservative.
**Latest SVG/PS vector-shading component exactness continuation:** native SVG/PostScript simple-shading replay now requires exact finite sampled function output arity for `/DeviceGray`, `/DeviceRGB`, `/DeviceCMYK`, `/CalGray`, `/CalRGB`, and `/Lab`. Overlong or non-finite vector-shading outputs remain on the conservative SVG/PS raster fallback boundary instead of being truncated into gradients or `shfill`.
**Latest SVG/PS Indexed constant shading-color continuation:** active shading validation, named-color resolution, and SVG/PostScript simple-shading replay now accept strict `/Indexed` color spaces with DeviceGray, DeviceRGB, DeviceCMYK, CalGray, CalRGB, Lab, Separation, DeviceN, default-qcms Gray/RGB ICCBased, and native-LittleCMS CMYK ICCBased bases when the sampled index component is exact, finite, integral, and constant across native stops. Malformed palettes, unsupported remaining Indexed bases, non-integer or overlong index components, and non-constant Indexed transitions remain typed refusals or conservative whole-page fallbacks instead of black/default conversion or inaccurate smooth native gradients.
**Latest SVG/PS named-color tint-transform shading continuation:** direct `/Separation` and one-colorant `/DeviceN` vector-shading replay now preflights named-color tint transforms before converting sampled tints into RGB stops. Native SVG gradients remain limited to linear Type 2 tint transforms with `N 1` and direct device-family alternates. PostScript `shfill` now has a separate exact named-color path: if the source shading tint function and the Separation/single-colorant DeviceN tint transform serialize as finite Type 2/Type 3 PostScript functions, `render/postscript.rs` emits the native named color space instead of endpoint RGB flattening. Multi-input DeviceN calculator transforms, malformed functions, unsupported alternates, unresolved transform metadata, and SVG nonlinear tint transforms remain whole-page fallbacks.
**Latest SVG/PS DCT Separation inline-image continuation:** regional inline-image classification now admits DCT terminal inline images whose resolved resource color space is an opaque-paintable `/Separation` tint space.
