Decoded JPEG output must still match the declared single tint channel; `/DeviceN` multi-component terminal-codec inline images and non-DCT tint-space terminal-codec inline images remain conservative whole-page fallbacks.
**Latest SVG/PS DCT one-colorant DeviceN inline-image continuation:** regional inline-image classification now admits DCT terminal inline images whose resolved resource color space is an opaque-paintable one-colorant `/DeviceN` tint space. Decoded JPEG output must still match the declared single tint channel; multi-component `/DeviceN` terminal-codec inline images and non-DCT tint-space terminal-codec inline images remain conservative whole-page fallbacks.
**Latest DCT inline ColorSpace guard continuation:** the legacy inline `DCTDecode` path now routes through the checked DCT finalizer, so JPEG component counts must match the declared inline image `ColorSpace` before `RawImage` output is returned.
**Latest image normalized-length continuation:** `build_raw_image` now validates normalized sample-buffer length before color conversion, and the DCT CMYK finalizer shortcut applies decode-budget and exact-length checks before decode-array application or CMYK-to-RGB conversion.
**Latest CMYK converter length continuation:** DeviceCMYK and ICCBased-CMYK image conversion entry points now validate exact input length before calling the chunked CMYK-to-RGB converter.
**Latest color-glyph JPEG invariant continuation:** decoded JPEG color-glyph output now fails typed when dimensions exceed the color-glyph cap, channel counts are unsupported, or decoded byte length does not match dimensions and channels before `RawImage` return or CMYK conversion.
**Latest color-glyph PNG invariant continuation:** decoded PNG color-glyph output now validates dimensions, channel count, and exact byte length before `RawImage` return; grayscale-alpha samples are checked as two-channel data before RGBA expansion.
**Latest color-glyph bitmap exactness continuation:** BGRA, gray8, gray subbyte, and mono bitmap color-glyph decoders now require exact payload lengths instead of slicing overlong glyph data down to the computed image length.
**Latest sub-byte row exactness continuation:** decoded 1/2/4 bpc image data now must match the computed packed row-byte length exactly before unpacking, so trailing decoded bytes no longer disappear before `RawImage` construction.
**Latest raw window source-length continuation:** unfiltered 1/2/4/8/16-bit raw source-window decode now requires the full source image stream length to match dimensions and channels exactly before cropping a requested window.
**Latest raw window bit-depth continuation:** guarded axis-aligned unfiltered raw Image XObjects and inline images now use bounded source-window decode for 1/2/4/8/16 bpc samples when no unsupported explicit mask, SMask, reduction, full-image postprocessing, or non-axis transform requires full-image materialization.
**Latest raw ImageMask source-window continuation:** guarded axis-aligned unfiltered `/ImageMask true` Image XObjects and inline images now use bounded 1 bpc source-window decode before stencil-color expansion when no reduction, full-image postprocessing, or non-axis transform requires full-image materialization.
**Latest raw explicit Mask source-window continuation:** guarded axis-aligned unfiltered raw Image XObjects with color-key `/Mask` arrays or same-sized referenced 1 bpc stencil `/Mask` streams now crop the main image and mask through the same source-window boundary when no `/SMask`, reduction, unsupported mask shape, or non-axis transform requires full-image materialization.
**Latest image SMask source-window continuation:** guarded axis-aligned unfiltered raw Image XObjects and monochrome CCITT Image XObjects with same-sized unfiltered grayscale `/SMask` streams now crop the main image and soft mask through the same source-window boundary. The CCITT path is limited to terminal `CCITTFaxDecode`/`CCF` with only known lossless stream wrappers, no inline/mask/smask image role, and `DeviceGray`/`G` metadata. Unsafe filtered, mismatched, non-grayscale, non-CCITT/non-raw, reduced, non-axis, or postprocessed soft-mask cases remain on the full-image/typed boundary. Evidence: `ccitt_smask_plan_uses_windowed_source_region_when_crop_aligned`, `image_smask_shared_source_window_allows_guarded_ccitt_main_image`, and `smask_loader_crops_source_window_for_ccitt_main_image`.
**Latest Indexed lookup exactness continuation:** image and active color `/Indexed` lookup tables now must match `(hival + 1) * base_channels` exactly; overlong lookup tables fail typed instead of being prefix-truncated.
**Latest Indexed arity exactness continuation:** image and active color `/Indexed` color-space arrays now must contain exactly four entries; overlong arrays fail typed instead of carrying ignored trailing metadata.
**Latest calibrated arity exactness continuation:** image, active color, and SVG/PS vector-shading CalGray/CalRGB/Lab arrays now must contain exactly two entries before parameter parsing or regional vector classification.
**Latest sbix parser fail-closed continuation:** color-glyph sbix strike metadata now fails typed for truncated strike headers, glyph-offset tables, malformed records, zero pixels-per-em, and malformed duplicate records instead of defaulting or disappearing as absent glyph data.
**Latest PDF function input-arity continuation:** PDF function evaluation now rejects missing/non-finite caller inputs and malformed required Type 0/2/3/4 function shapes instead of evaluating with implicit zero inputs or default required fields.
**Latest SMask transfer backdrop continuation:** alpha soft-mask backdrop probing now validates `/TR` before testing `TR(0)` and fails typed for malformed or empty-output transfer functions instead of treating them as transparent-at-zero.
**Latest transaction dependency-retention continuation:** narrow `RenderDocumentCache` invalidations now keep existing source/page/tile dependency edges across successful revision advancement. `RenderDependencyGraph::advance_revision` updates the revision without clearing edges, while `reset_revision` remains the destructive reset path. Sequential source-only edits now continue to invalidate the second source's recorded page/tile dependencies instead of losing the graph after the first narrow edit and forcing a conservative reset.
**Latest transaction dirty-tile coverage continuation:** exact dirty-tile invalidation is now selected only when the provided valid tile set covers every reported affected page, and the dependency graph now expands recorded page tiles for any source-derived invalidated page whose exact/source-tile set does not cover every recorded rendered tile for that page. Binding-safe invalidation plans and direct `TransactionWriteSet` application ignore page-0 or zero-sized exact-tile entries for coverage/application and expand page raster invalidation for uncovered pages, preventing stale page tiles when a multi-page edit carries exact dirty tiles for only one viewport, malformed exact tiles, or a changed source maps to a page with no or partial exact source-tile coverage. SDK render-invalidation reports expose `exact_tile_coverage_complete` and `uncovered_affected_pages`.
**Latest transaction nested write-set continuation:** cache-plan application now scans SDK/server/report envelopes for nested render write-set fields, maps PDF refs, structured `[object, generation]`/`{object_number, generation}`/`{ref}` refs, and `object-*`/`stream-*` refs through `RenderDocumentCache`'s remembered object identities, merges the resulting source IDs and marker entries into the plan, and conservatively resets when a nested ref cannot be mapped. This lets DocumentSubsystems and secure-mutation field/widget/AP reports prune exact source tiles and source-bound artifacts even when callers hand the cache owner a full apply-with-render-invalidation envelope instead of a pre-expanded `mapped_source_ids` list.
**Latest transaction structured shared-resource continuation:** the same envelope scanner now normalizes structured nested write-set refs, including `[object, generation]` arrays, `{object_number, generation}` objects, and `{ref}`/`{object_ref}`/`{source_ref}` records, before applying a plan to `RenderDocumentCache`. Known structured font/image/Form/shading/pattern/resource refs merge into mapped source IDs and source-cache marker entries; unknown structured refs still force conservative reset. Evidence: `nested_write_set_collector_accepts_structured_object_refs`, `render_invalidation_plan_json_maps_structured_shared_resource_refs`, and `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine transaction_invalidation --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`.
**Latest SVG shading BBox clip continuation:** SVG native shading and shading-pattern replay now fails typed when an accepted shading `/BBox` cannot be flattened into a device-space clip, instead of installing an empty clip path and silently dropping the paint.
**Latest overprint CMYK state exactness continuation:** overprint-preview CMYK row dispatch now requires exactly four finite active or retained DeviceCMYK components before preview blending instead of padding malformed internal color state with zero inks.
**Latest utility image sample-length continuation:** utility image ingestion now validates exact decoded sample length before JPEG CMYK conversion, PNG grayscale/grayscale-alpha/RGB/RGBA expansion, and rendered-page RGBA/gray-to-RGB conversion.
**Latest SVG/PS monochrome terminal inline-image classifier continuation:** regional inline-image classification now keeps CCITT/JBIG2 terminal inline images declared as `DeviceRGB` or `DeviceCMYK` on the whole-page fallback boundary because those decoders return monochrome/grayscale samples without applying multi-component device color semantics. `DeviceGray` CCITT/JBIG2 terminal inline images remain regionally eligible.
**Latest monochrome terminal image decode guard continuation:** CCITT/JBIG2 terminal image decode now rejects non-`DeviceGray`/`G` declared or resolved image color spaces before full inline decode, Image XObject decode, inline CCITT source-window decode, and Image XObject CCITT source-window decode can return monochrome samples to a multi-component image path.
**Latest SVG/PS JPX internal-alpha classifier continuation:** PostScript regional Image XObject and inline-image classification now defers terminal JPX images with declared `/SMaskInData 1` or `/SMaskInData 2` to decode/emission validation instead of rejecting them purely from metadata. SVG still admits the same metadata for regional PNG alpha embedding; PostScript keeps decoded opaque, fully transparent, or binary 0/255 alpha regional, while decoded fractional alpha remains a whole-page raster fallback.
**Latest PostScript binary regional alpha continuation:** decoded PostScript regional RGBA now distinguishes opaque, fully transparent, binary 0/255, and fractional alpha. Fully transparent regions are no-ops, binary 0/255 regions are clipped through bounded device-space cell paths before `colorimage`, and fractional alpha still falls back through whole-page raster output.
**Latest PostScript exact Type 3 stitching continuation:** PostScript axial/radial shading classification now carries an exact FunctionType 3 sidecar for direct DeviceRGB/DeviceGray, unit-domain stitching functions whose Type 2 segments can be represented natively as RGB, collapses direct DeviceRGB Type 2 function arrays to one exact RGB Type 2 sidecar when every component shares the same finite unit-domain exponent, carries mixed-exponent direct DeviceRGB Type 2 function arrays as exact per-component PostScript function arrays, carries direct DeviceCMYK Type 2 functions as exact `/ColorSpace /DeviceCMYK` sidecars, and carries direct DeviceCMYK Type 2 function arrays as either collapsed exact CMYK sidecars or exact `/ColorSpace /DeviceCMYK` per-component PostScript function arrays. The PostScript sink serializes `/Functions`, `/Bounds`, and `/Encode` directly for stitching, `/FunctionType 2 /N` for same-exponent arrays, per-channel `/FunctionType 2` dictionaries for mixed-exponent arrays, and exact CMYK `/C0`, `/C1`, and `/N` values for direct CMYK; conservative/SVG targets, calibrated/named or otherwise inexact nonlinear functions remain fail-closed instead of being approximated from sampled stops.
**Latest JPX dimension-consistency continuation:** every JPX terminal decode path now routes through a checked finalizer that rejects decoded JPX dimensions differing from the PDF inline image or Image XObject dictionary `/Width` and `/Height` before `/SMaskInData` handling or downstream paint/cache behavior.
**Latest JPX SMaskInData metadata continuation:** JPX finalization now validates `/SMaskInData` as integer `0`, `1`, or `2`, and non-zero declarations require decoded JPX output with an alpha channel. Malformed metadata or declared internal soft masks without decoded alpha now fail typed instead of being ignored.
**Latest JPX finalizer invariant continuation:** JPX finalization now rejects non-8-bit samples, zero-channel decoded output, decoded byte-length mismatches, and decoded dimensions over the image decode budget before `/SMaskInData` handling or downstream paint/cache behavior.
**Latest SVG/PS tiling-pattern Matrix continuation:** focused SVG/PostScript fixtures now cover finite invertible affine `/Matrix` metadata on the supported simple colored tiling-pattern subset. The existing replay path applies the pattern matrix before the active page CTM, enumerates visible cells through that matrix-bearing CTM, and replays native tiles with translated matrix-bearing CTMs plus per-tile BBox clips; nested pattern paint, transparency-bearing cells, over-cap tiling, malformed/non-invertible matrices, and resource programs outside the vector-safe image/Form/shading/inline-image subset remain conservative.
**Latest portable qcms CMYK admission continuation:** the default portable qcms ICC path now rejects CMYK ICC profiles before transform construction and records an unsupported profile outcome instead of panicking in debug qcms builds. CMYK ICC profile-to-sRGB remains on the native LittleCMS feature path.
**Latest SVG/PS shading-pattern stencil-mask continuation:** SVG regional fallback now accepts simple shading-pattern painted Image XObject and inline-image stencil masks by emitting a decoded SVG mask plus the existing native gradient fill. PostScript now accepts bounded versions of the same shape by vectorizing painted stencil cells into a native clip path before replaying shading-pattern `shfill`; larger masks stay conservative under the vectorized-cell cap.
**Latest SVG/PS colored/uncolored tiling-pattern continuation:** SVG/PostScript regional fallback now accepts simple colored and uncolored `/PatternType 1` tiling-pattern path paints when the pattern stream decodes completely, carries exact finite geometry metadata, uses `/PaintType` 1 or 2, has positive tile steps, carries an absent or finite invertible affine `/Matrix`, and its tile program is pure vector or contains scoped vector-safe image/Form/shading/inline-image resources without nested pattern paint. `/PaintType 2` additionally requires finite caller DeviceGray, DeviceRGB, or DeviceCMYK components and rejects tile streams that set paint color themselves. SVG and PostScript clip the painted path, apply the pattern matrix before the active page CTM, enumerate visible cells through the matrix-bearing CTM, apply per-tile BBox clips, and replay bounded tiles as native vector paths under `MAX_VECTOR_TILING_PATTERN_CELLS`; nested pattern-paint, color-setting uncolored, negative-step, over-cap, malformed/non-invertible matrix, transparency-bearing, resource programs outside the vector-safe image/Form/shading/inline-image subset, and advanced tiling patterns remain conservative.
**Latest SVG/PS tiling-pattern stencil-mask continuation:** SVG/PostScript regional fallback now accepts inline-image and Image XObject stencil masks painted with the supported colored/uncolored tiling-pattern subset. SVG wraps native tile replay in the decoded stencil mask; PostScript reuses the vectorized stencil-cell clip and native tile replay. The path remains bounded by the same stream, geometry, caller-color, and visible-cell guards as tiling-pattern path paints.
**Latest SVG/PS tiling-pattern text continuation:** SVG/PostScript regional fallback now accepts glyph-outline text fill/stroke painted with the supported colored/uncolored tiling-pattern subset. Both sinks try bounded native tile replay for glyph fill/stroke outlines before falling back to the existing shading-pattern text replay.
**Latest SVG/PS resource-bearing tiling-cell continuation:** SVG/PostScript regional fallback now threads viewport scale and the active Form recursion stack into tiling-pattern classification, then replays each tile with the pattern's merged resources and nested regional allow-lists. Colored tiling cells containing vector-safe Image XObjects, Form XObjects, named shadings, or complete inline images can remain bounded/native; nested `/Pattern` color spaces, unsupported resource programs, and color-setting uncolored cells still fail closed.
**Latest exact raster text metric-source continuation:** high-quality/exact raster text now advances only from PDF widths, native Type 3 metrics, or strict variation-aware sfnt/bare-CFF font metrics. Metricless exact raster glyphs record a typed `HighQualityExact` refusal instead of using the compatibility `500` horizontal fallback, and vertical writing requires a real vertical CMap advance instead of `-1000`.

**Latest compact group compositor oracle continuation:** compact `composite_from_at` non-normal and high-quality fallback compositing now delegates to the same `blend_pixel` scalar oracle used by full-page group flattening, with active page SMask temporarily detached so caller-provided soft masks are not multiplied twice. This closes the compact-only preblend divergence for non-normal blend modes, non-separable modes, and high-quality gamma handling.
**Latest display-list CPU path-mask cache continuation:** direct CPU fill and stroke path-mask caches now use byte-accounted LRU admission/eviction. Hits refresh recency, replacement updates byte charges, and entry/byte pressure evicts only the least-recently-used masks instead of clearing the complete cache.
**Latest packed-plan missing-resource continuation:** packed Image XObject, Form XObject, and named shading descriptors now fail closed during `compile_with_resources` when the named resource is absent from the available resource table. Execution dispatches a typed compile refusal instead of falling back to runtime name lookup, and the replay adapter itself now refuses unresolved Image/Form/shading/ExtGState/font/named-color-space/named-pattern/OC-property descriptors rather than performing late resource-map lookup if a stale or manually built plan reaches execution.
**Latest retained subprogram exactness continuation:** cached Form XObject, transparency Form group, annotation appearance, tiling-pattern, and soft-mask `/G` Form subprograms now store or compute retained-plan refusal reasons when retained compilation is unavailable or emits a packed compile refusal. High-quality/exact and compatibility rendering refuse those unsupported retained subprograms before raw immediate replay can run. Evidence: `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine unsupported_retained --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`.
**Latest Type 3 cache-reason continuation:** parsed Type 3 CharProc and path-geometry cache misses now store the exact source-collection failure reason for unresolved `/CharProcs`, decode failures, byte/op caps, parse failures, unsupported path extraction, or empty geometry instead of caching a generic unavailable entry. Evidence: `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine type3 --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`.
**Latest SMask cache identity continuation:** soft-mask `/G` cache identity now salts the structural clip-state fingerprint, active color-management policy, and active overprint policy. Distinct clips with identical visible bounds can no longer share cached masks, and CMM/overprint policy changes no longer rely only on the broader render-contract string. Evidence: `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine smask_group_cache_key_includes_contract_output_identity --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`.
**Latest transparent-page-group cache identity continuation:** top-level transparent-page-group decisions are now keyed by page, document revision, and the active render-contract fingerprint while preserving page-prefixed invalidation. Evidence: `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine transparent_page_group --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`.
**Verification policy:** historical VPS evidence is retained where labelled, but this continuation is local-only source work; no VPS, SSH, corpus campaign, performance benchmark, competitor comparison, deployment, release tag, or package publication.

## Status meaning

`COMPLETE_ACTIVE` requires active source integration, a test/smoke result, bounded resources where applicable, and no placeholder claim. `PARTIAL` means production code exists but one or more universal requirements remain unclosed. `EXPLICIT_REFUSAL` is preferable to degraded pixels when exact rendering cannot be provided.

RV-18/RV-36 function, dictionary-field, mesh-decode, and vector-geometry update: active shading validation now uses strict PDF function-shape checks before sampling Type 0, Type 2, Type 3, and Type 4 functions, and it validates present shading `/Coords`, `/Domain`, `/Extend`, Type 1 `/Matrix`, Type 4-7 mesh bit-depth fields, exact finite mesh `/Decode` cardinality, and Type 5 `/VerticesPerRow` before paint. Malformed present function arrays, missing Type 0 `/BitsPerSample`, shading dictionaries, mesh decode fields, and short Type 0 sampled-function streams fail typed instead of being filtered into shorter numeric arrays, silently defaulted, defaulted to 8-bit Type 0 samples, or sampled with missing bytes as zero. SVG/PS simple shading classification rejects malformed `/Coords`, optional `/BBox`, non-default or malformed `/Domain`, malformed `/Extend`, and malformed Type 2 function arrays before native vector replay. Focused evidence includes `mesh_decode_rejects_invalid_bit_fields`, `mesh_decode_rejects_non_numeric_or_short_decode_arrays`, `malformed_shading_geometry_stays_whole_page_fallback`, `function_shape_rejects_malformed_present_type2_arrays`, `type0_rejects_missing_bits_per_sample_instead_of_defaulting`, `type0_rejects_short_sample_stream_instead_of_zero_padding`, `malformed_active_shading_dictionary_returns_typed_refusal`, `malformed_shading_pattern_returns_typed_refusal`, `malformed_function_array_shading_stays_whole_page_fallback`, `function`, and `shading`.

RV-22/RV-36 update: the 2026-08-18 local continuation extends image-decode/fallback evidence to inline image sequencing, active image XObject metadata, image locator/extraction metadata, referenced mask metadata, and uncolored tiling-pattern color counts. Malformed inline image sequences, including data without `ID`, `ID` without `BI`, dangling `BI`, pending `ID`, missing data bytes, and payloads without closing `EI`, fail typed/unsupported before retained or immediate rendering can fabricate empty params, paint unterminated bytes, or silently drop the image. Missing or invalid XObject dimensions, missing/unsupported non-mask bpc outside terminal JPX, and missing/malformed non-mask color-space metadata fail typed before active image-reference/cache-key construction or decode planning, and the public `ImageLocator` now applies matching fail-closed metadata rules before exposing image inventory, extraction, or decode-capability references. Referenced explicit `/Mask` streams and image SMask streams now fail typed on missing/invalid dimensions, bpc, or color-space metadata before mask decode. PaintType 2 tiling patterns now fail typed on unexpected active pattern-color component counts before tile replay, with focused `malformed_inline_image_sequence_marks_display_list_unsupported`, `malformed_inline_image_sequence_returns_typed_refusal`, `inline_image_triggers_whole_page`, `inline_image`, `renderer_image_xobject_`, `locator`, `image_locator`, `extract_image_bytes`, `locator_captures`, `image_decode_capability_report`, `image_xobject_explicit_mask_missing`, `image_xobject_smask_missing`, `smask_`, `uncolored_tiling_pattern_invalid_component_count`, `tiling_pattern`, and `image_xobject` evidence.

RV-34/RV-36 regional image metadata update: SVG/PS vector-output classification and sink reference construction now require regional Image XObjects to expose a resolved stream dictionary with positive dimensions, supported non-mask `/BitsPerComponent` metadata outside terminal JPX, explicit non-mask `/ColorSpace`, strict stencil-mask `/BitsPerComponent 1` when present, well-formed stencil `/Decode`, and boolean-valued present `/ImageMask`/`/IM` or `/Interpolate`/`/I` metadata. Resource-named Image XObject color spaces that pass preflight are decoded through the resolved color-space object instead of a default sink reference. Regional inline images use the same fail-closed positive-dimension, non-mask BPC, non-mask ColorSpace, filter, DecodeParms, stencil Decode, and boolean metadata preflight before native SVG/PS embedding. Malformed regional image metadata now routes to the existing whole-page typed raster/refusal boundary instead of being emitted as a bounded native image with defaulted dimensions, bpc, color space, booleans, or mask polarity. Focused evidence includes `classifier_malformed_image_xobject_boolean_metadata_stays_whole_page`, `malformed_image_xobject_metadata_triggers_whole_page`, `classifier_malformed_inline_image_metadata_stays_whole_page`, `image_xobject_resource_color_space`, `regional_vector_fallback`, `vector_fallback`, `image_xobject`, and `inline_image`.

RV-34/RV-36 inline parameter-pair update: inline-image `ID` parameter normalization, active renderer helper construction, and SVG/PS regional classification now reject non-name keys, dangling keys, duplicate canonical keys, and short/long alias duplicates before decode planning or native regional output. Malformed parameter pairs no longer collapse into smaller dictionaries through tolerant normalization. Focused evidence includes `malformed_inline_image_parameters_return_parse_error`, `malformed_inline_image_parameters_return_typed_refusal`, `renderer_inline_image_malformed_parameter_pairs_return_typed_refusal`, and `malformed_inline_image_parameters_stay_whole_page_fallback`.

RV-20/RV-36 update: Type 3 path-only geometry extraction now refuses wrong-count, nonnumeric, non-finite, or invalid operands for supported path construction, transform, line-state, dash, color, and metric operators, and refuses `l/c/v/y` without a current point. Focused evidence includes `type3_path_collector_rejects_malformed_graphics_state_operands`, `type3_path_collector_rejects_path_segments_without_current_point`, `type3_clipping_charproc_malformed_line_width_returns_typed_refusal`, and the `type3` focused test group.

RV-20/RV-36 metric update: generic Type 3 `d0`/`d1` metric operands now use `type3_glyph_metric_operand_refusal` before retained descriptor compilation, display-list capture, immediate page dispatch, or SVG/PS vector-output classification. Malformed glyph displacement/bbox operands fail typed or unsupported instead of producing zero-width/zero-bbox descriptors or malformed native vector output. Focused evidence includes `type3_glyph_metric_operand_validator_rejects_malformed_operands`, `malformed_type3_glyph_metric_marks_display_list_unsupported`, `malformed_type3_glyph_metric_operator_returns_typed_refusal`, and `malformed_operands_stay_whole_page_vector_fallback`.

RV-08/RV-36 update: active non-text graphics-state operands now use `graphics_state_operand_refusal` before retained descriptor compilation, display-list capture, immediate page rendering, and SVG/PS vector-output classification. Malformed operands for `q/Q`, transforms, line state, dash state, rendering intent, flatness, direct color, color-space, pattern-name, and ExtGState operators fail typed instead of applying identity/default state or replaying malformed vector state; `Q` restore underflow also fails before display-list/page rendering or stream/Form-local vector classification can call the tolerant empty-stack restore path. Focused evidence includes `graphics_state_descriptor_rejects_malformed_state_operands`, `malformed_graphics_state_marks_display_list_unsupported`, `malformed_graphics_state_operator_returns_typed_refusal`, `malformed_operands_stay_whole_page_vector_fallback`, `graphics_state_descriptor`, and `graphics_state`.

RV-08/RV-36 text update: active text-state and text-showing operands now use `text_operand_refusal` before retained text capture, text-state descriptor compilation, immediate page text rendering, and SVG/PS vector-output classification. Malformed operands fail typed instead of applying empty strings, empty arrays, ignored `TJ` entries, zero text state, default font size, identity matrices, or malformed vector text; nested `BT`, unmatched `ET`, text showing/positioning outside `BT`/`ET`, and unterminated `BT` also fail before display-list capture, immediate/packed text replay, or vector sink replay can tolerate malformed text-object state. Focused evidence includes `text_operand_validator_rejects_malformed_text_operands`, `malformed_text_marks_display_list_unsupported`, `malformed_text_operator_returns_typed_refusal`, `malformed_operands_stay_whole_page_vector_fallback`, `text_is_replayable_as_native_operation`, and the `text` focused test group.

RV-08/RV-36 marked-content update: active marked-content, property-list, and compatibility-section operands now use `marked_content_operand_refusal` before retained descriptor compilation, display-list capture, immediate optional-content visibility mutation, and SVG/PS vector-output classification. Display-list finish, relative `RenderState::dispatch_all` boundaries, and vector classification reject unmatched `EMC`, cross-stream `EMC`, unmatched `EX`, and unterminated `BMC`/`BDC`/`BX` begins. Malformed operands and unbalanced sequences fail typed instead of applying empty tags, empty property lists, ignored operands, default visible state, leaked visibility, unbalanced compatibility-section no-ops, or malformed native vector replay. Focused evidence includes `marked_content_operand_validator_rejects_malformed_operands`, `malformed_marked_content_marks_display_list_unsupported`, `unterminated_marked_content_marks_display_list_unsupported`, `unbalanced_compatibility_section_marks_display_list_unsupported`, `malformed_marked_content_operator_returns_typed_refusal`, `unterminated_marked_content_operator_returns_typed_refusal`, `unbalanced_compatibility_section_returns_typed_refusal`, `malformed_operands_stay_whole_page_vector_fallback`, and the `marked_content` focused test group.

RV-08/RV-36 path update: active page path construction, paint, and clip operands now use `path_operand_refusal` before display-list capture, immediate path mutation, or SVG/PS vector-output classification. Malformed operands, continuation operators without active current points, dangling `W/W*`, and repeated `W/W*` fail typed instead of dropping construction input, painting/clipping with operand-bearing operators, synthesizing implicit moves, treating empty clips as no-ops, or emitting malformed native vector paths. Valid empty or move-only clipping paths install an explicit empty clip through retained and immediate replay. Focused evidence includes `path_operand_validator_rejects_malformed_operands`, `malformed_path_marks_display_list_unsupported`, `malformed_path_operator_returns_typed_refusal`, `empty_path_clip_installs_empty_clip`, `malformed_operands_stay_whole_page_vector_fallback`, and the `path` focused test group.

RV-08/RV-36 resource invocation update: active XObject and shading invocation operands now use `resource_invocation_operand_refusal` before display-list capture, immediate resource dispatch, or SVG/PS vector-output classification. Missing, wrong-type, or extra operands for `Do` and `sh` fail typed or force the typed raster path instead of no-oping, reaching empty-name lookup, regionalizing malformed output, or dropping trailing operands. Focused evidence includes `resource_invocation_operand_validator_rejects_malformed_operands`, `malformed_resource_invocation_marks_display_list_unsupported`, `malformed_resource_invocation_operator_returns_typed_refusal`, `malformed_resource_invocation_stays_whole_page_vector_fallback`, `xobject_resource`, and `named_shading`.

## Implemented source guards and compact tests

- Canonical view isolation and source identity: `render/document_view.rs` tests.
- Contract schema/identity: `render/contract.rs` tests and C ABI round-trip test.
- Packed plan equality, spatial order, diagnostics-only cold-table guard, typed retained text, typed retained inline images, and typed resource-name retained ops: `render/plan.rs` and `render/display_list.rs` tests.
- Revision/tile invalidation model: `render/invalidation.rs` source-page/source-tile/explicit-tile tests, `render/transaction_invalidation.rs` source-ID mapping/page-union/dirty-region-conversion/explicit-tile tests, and `render/page_renderer.rs` bounded Form XObject, text font-reference, and typed pattern-state tile-edge extraction tests.
- Progressive request cancellation, cancellable queue/prefetch execution, and viewport obsolete-publication reports: linked `CancelToken` parents, session-owned request-cancel, C ABI declaration, binding source surfaces, cancellable C/Python/WASM/.NET/Java viewer-queue and adjacent-page prefetch execution surfaces, and bounded `obsolete_publications` JSON after viewport revision.
- Render-time font substitution report parity: C ABI/Python/WASM/.NET/Java source render APIs return bounded report JSON for fast PNG, contract PNG, and caller-owned contract surfaces; server contract PNG/raw report routes return multipart metadata/report plus rendered bytes.
- Progressive lifecycle: `render/progressive.rs` test plus existing page renderer equivalence tests.
- Cache identity and fallback source guards: `renderer_architecture_guards.rs` and `display_list` cache-key test.
- Cache resource-budget enforcement: `render_document_cache_enforces_aggregate_resource_budget`, `render_document_cache_byte_charges_path_clip_nodes`, image/mesh per-cache LRU, Form/tiling/annotation-appearance program, font-map, display-list byte-budget tests, and runtime capability disclosure that names transformed path clip-node accounting.
- Pattern typed refusal, Type 3 native charproc plus missing-name typed refusal, Type 3 parsed-program LRU cache admission, named shading diagnostic, and PS ExtGState safety: focused renderer tests.
- Persistent clip-DAG representation variants and metadata: focused `clip_dag` unit tests.
- Separable solid-fill source-alpha and cached RGBA, including mixed-alpha/mixed-destination RGBA no-clip, binary-clip, and partial-clip f32x4 row fusion: focused `separable_rgba_pixels_at` / `separable_` unit tests.
- Portable-wide opaque-destination separable fill rows now cover all PDF separable modes: focused `separable_extended_modes_fill_rect_uses_wide_row_path` and `separable_difference_exclusion_fill_rect_uses_wide_row_path` unit tests.
- Compact group non-normal/high-quality scalar oracle fallback: focused `compact_` and `composite_from_at` unit tests.
- Direct PDFium C harness source guard, including form-fill API wiring and typed unsupported form-policy refusal, plus compact visual-diff JSON/schema tests; no PDFium SDK runtime or corpus execution in this local pass.
- CLI render-contract raw surface, sidecar, and JSON input replay path: `render_contract_raw_surface_and_sidecar_runs`.
- SVG/PS vector-safe Form, inline-image, simple axial/radial-shading, and ExtGState font/flatness regionalization: `regional_vector_fallback` integration test.

## Honest conclusion

The pass materially advances the renderer architecture and removes several avoidable degraded fallbacks. It does **not** establish universal renderer closure: high-level packed plans, complete invalidation from edits, all SIMD targets, full clip-DAG hot replay/fusion, full progressive binding sessions, full print execution, remaining SVG/PS regional fallback constructs, complete image/JPX decode, and complete binding contract parity remain unfinished. No benchmark-readiness claim is made by this report.

Simple linear DeviceGray/DeviceRGB/DeviceCMYK plus direct/resource-resolved well-formed CalGray/CalRGB/Lab/Separation axial shadings with preserved supported `/Extend` clips, endpoint-sampled contained non-unit `/Domain` support, function-domain-clipped stop-list support, and transformed shading `/BBox` clipping, plus finite/invertible affine-transformed DeviceGray/DeviceRGB/DeviceCMYK plus direct/resource-resolved well-formed CalGray/CalRGB/Lab/Separation radial shadings including finite concentric non-default `/Extend` clips through the same simple Type 2 endpoint-sampling, continuous Type 3 stitching stop-list, function-domain clipping, and BBox path, are emitted as SVG `<linearGradient>`/`<radialGradient>` fills and PostScript/EPS LanguageLevel 3 `DeviceRGB shfill` dictionaries; malformed calibrated shading dictionaries no longer use default Cal/Lab parameters for native vector gradients. Simple finite-matrix `/PatternType 2` shading-pattern fill/stroke path paints and glyph-outline text paints reuse that vector-safe shading subset through clipped SVG gradient geometry and clipped PostScript/EPS `shfill`, intersecting the shading BBox clip when present. Resolved line-style/dash/font/flatness/smoothness/default-state/identity-transfer/first-supported-normal-blend-array ExtGState dictionaries are applied natively to paths and stroked/fill-stroke text outlines, including finite non-negative dash arrays with finite non-negative phases, finite non-negative `/Font [/F size]` entries, active finite non-negative `FL` path flatness, active finite non-negative `SM` Coons/tensor patch-mesh subdivision tolerance, default `OP false`/`op false`/valid `OPM 0/1`/`SA false`/`AIS false`/`TK true`, normal or first-supported normal-compatible `BM`, and `TR`/`TR2` set to `/Identity` or four-`/Identity` arrays; SVG additionally accepts finite normal-blend `CA`/`ca` opacity and emits native opacity attributes, while PostScript keeps visible fractional-alpha paint on the fallback boundary, skips zero-alpha paint as an exact no-op, and skips fully transparent regional RGBA buffers before `colorimage`, and clips binary 0/255 regional RGBA before `colorimage`; SVG stacked clips compose by referencing the previous active `clipPath` in the child clip definition; font-resolved text-clipping render modes 4-7 accumulate glyph outlines into native SVG `clipPath` / PostScript `clip` state; overwritten/dead pattern color state no longer triggers whole-page fallback. Advanced pattern paint and resource-bearing tiling cells outside the vector-safe image/Form/shading/inline-image subset, unsupported advanced pattern-space transforms, active patterned text outside the supported shading/tiling glyph-outline subset, unsupported radial Extend geometry, malformed calibrated shading dictionaries, malformed or degenerate shading BBoxes, alpha/backdrop-observable semantic transparency groups, alpha/backdrop-observable or richer non-device group-colour transparency Forms, visible PostScript fractional-alpha/non-normal-blend paint, fractional regional RGBA, SVG soft masks or semantic transparency groups outside native blend/opacity, visible PostScript non-normal-blend paint, soft masks, non-identity transfer functions, invalid dash/font arrays, enabled overprint, invalid overprint mode, `SA true`, `AIS true`, `TK false`, unresolved/preflight-unavailable text clipping, unknown ExtGState keys, degenerate image transforms, active pattern-painted stencil masks outside the supported shading/tiling subset, `/None` or unresolved Separation/DeviceN/tint-space inline-image resources, multi-component DeviceN terminal-codec tint-space inline images, non-DCT terminal-codec tint-space inline images, CCITT/JBIG2 terminal inline images declared as non-gray device color spaces, non-DCT terminal-codec calibrated/Indexed/ICCBased inline images, and unsupported inline-image shapes still force whole-page raster embedding or typed refusal. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine calibrated_group_color_space --test regional_vector_fallback --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; | SVG/PS dense-text and regional-image continuation | Dense ordinary text still had a classifier operation-count whole-page fallback, regional Image XObject replay could lose source color-space/filter metadata, and an approved regional image/inline-image replay failure could silently omit the bounded image. Unsupported regional inline cases could also reach whole-page fallback where the active renderer treated resource-named inline `/CS0` as an unknown device color space. | Dense ordinary text no longer trips an operation-count whole-page fallback; SVG/PS regional Image XObject replay preserves source color-space/filter metadata; approved regional image/inline-image replay failures fail typed instead of silently omitting the bounded region; and active whole-page fallback resolves resource-named inline image color spaces before decode. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine active_renderer_inline_image_resource_indexed_color_space_resolves_named_resource --test regional_vector_fallback --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine --test regional_vector_fallback --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine raster_fallback_triggers_on_unsupported_constructs --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1 -- -D warnings`; `cargo check --workspace --all-targets --jobs 1` |
| SVG/PS shading-pattern regionalization | Active shading-pattern fill/stroke path paints and text paints still forced whole-page SVG/PS raster output outside ordinary solid-color glyph outlines. | Simple finite-matrix `/PatternType 2` shading-pattern fill/stroke path paints and glyph-outline text paints now stay native: the classifier validates simple axial/finite/invertible affine-transformed radial shading dictionaries, SVG emits a clipped gradient rectangle, and PostScript/EPS emits clipped LanguageLevel 3 `shfill`; tiling patterns, unsupported advanced pattern-space transforms, patterned text outside the supported shading/tiling glyph-outline subset, stencils, transparency, and other unsupported pattern constructs remain whole-page fallback. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine simple_shading_pattern --test regional_vector_fallback --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine shading_pattern_text --test regional_vector_fallback --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine shading_pattern --test regional_vector_fallback --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine --test regional_vector_fallback --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture` |
| Active named-color tint/alternate fail-closed | Separation/DeviceN named-color resolution could pad missing tint components, force `/All` to full ink, or treat malformed alternate-space arrays as `/DeviceRGB`. | `render/colorspace.rs` now requires exact finite supplied Separation/DeviceN tint vectors, uses the supplied `/All` tint value, validates DeviceN component-name arrays before tint evaluation, and refuses malformed alternate-space metadata instead of falling through to black or `/DeviceRGB` component defaults. | `cargo fmt --all`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine colorspace --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine tint --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture` |
| Named-color component-vector exactness | The named-color resolver still used finite-prefix checks in several paths, so overlong bare device resources, CalGray/CalRGB/Lab vectors, Separation/DeviceN tint inputs, device alternate outputs, or ICCBased scalar inputs could be truncated after earlier missing-component checks passed. | `render/colorspace.rs` now requires exact finite counts for bare device named colors, calibrated named colors, Separation/DeviceN tint inputs, and device alternate outputs; `render/cmm.rs` requires ICCBased scalar vectors to match profile `/N` exactly. Overlong component vectors now produce typed invalid/unsupported named-color outcomes instead of prefix truncation. | `cargo fmt --all --check`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine colorspace --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine cmm --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine tint --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1 -- -D warnings`; `git diff --check -- crates/engine/src/render/colorspace.rs crates/engine/src/render/cmm.rs docs/renderer/final-local-implementation-closure.md docs/renderer/complete-algorithm-and-method-inventory.md docs/renderer/final-fallback-closure-report.md docs/renderer/final-fallback-inventory.md docs/renderer/final-universal-implementation-report.md docs/renderer/final-universal-renderer-implementation-report.md` |
| Named-color tint-transform shape fail-closed | Separation/DeviceN tint transforms reached the generic function evaluator without first enforcing the strict active-render function shape boundary, so malformed functions could fall through to evaluator defaults and multi-input DeviceN could accept single-input Function Type 2/3 shapes that ignore extra tint inputs. | `render/colorspace.rs` now validates each tint transform with `validate_function_shape` before evaluation and returns `NamedColor::Invalid("malformed tint transform")` for malformed or runtime-empty transform output. The named-color path also rejects Function Type 2 and Type 3 tint transforms when DeviceN supplies more than one tint input; generic function validation remains compatible with existing Type 1 shading behavior. | `cargo fmt --all`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine tint_transform --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine tint --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine --test shadings function_based_shading_type1_varies_with_x --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture` |
| DeviceN/Separation tint-transform cache | The ICC transform path had a bounded transform cache, but Separation/DeviceN tint functions were evaluated directly on every named-color resolution and had no inspectable colour-cache identity, admission, eviction, or document-boundary stats. | `render/colorspace.rs` now routes successful Separation/DeviceN tint-transform outputs through a thread-local entry/byte-capped LRU keyed by source document hash/length, resolved tint-function fingerprint, exact input count, and tint input bits. Invalid or oversized outputs are not admitted, DeviceN uses the validated input slice without allocating a copy, and `ColorReport` exposes tint cache metrics plus configured caps. | `cargo fmt --all --check`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine tint_transform_cache --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine tint_transform --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1 -- -D warnings` |
| Active shading ColorSpace fail-closed | Active shading validation checked required fields and functions but did not preflight the shading `/ColorSpace`, so unsupported spaces or malformed function outputs could reach generic component conversion. | `validate_shading_dictionary_for_paint` now validates `/ColorSpace` before paint and checks sampled function outputs against resolved device, calibrated, ICCBased, Separation, or DeviceN component counts. Unsupported/malformed color spaces and invalid vectors fail typed instead of converting through black/default component fallback. | `cargo fmt --all --check`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine malformed_active_shading_dictionary_returns_typed_refusal --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine shading --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1 -- -D warnings` |
| Active shading device-component exactness | The previous active shading device-color check rejected too-short and non-finite leading components but still allowed overlong `/DeviceGray`, `/DeviceRGB`, or `/DeviceCMYK` sampled function outputs to reach later truncating conversion. | `validate_device_color_components` now requires exact finite component counts for bare and array-wrapped device color spaces before active shading paint. Too-short, overlong, or non-finite device vectors record typed shading refusals. | `cargo fmt --all --check`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine malformed_active_shading_dictionary_returns_typed_refusal --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine shading --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1 -- -D warnings`; `git diff --check -- crates/engine/src/render/page_renderer.rs docs/renderer/final-local-implementation-closure.md docs/renderer/complete-algorithm-and-method-inventory.md docs/renderer/final-fallback-closure-report.md docs/renderer/final-fallback-inventory.md docs/renderer/final-universal-implementation-report.md docs/renderer/final-universal-renderer-implementation-report.md` |
| SVG-in-OpenType paint metadata fail-closed | Static SVG color-glyph parsing clamped out-of-range `rgb()` components, percentages, and opacity values into visible paint instead of treating malformed present paint metadata as invalid. | `parse_color_component` now rejects numeric components outside `0..=255` and percentages outside `0..=100`; `parse_unit_interval` now rejects opacity outside `0..=1`. Omitted optional SVG defaults remain valid. | `cargo fmt --all --check`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine svg_static_subset --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine color_glyph --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1 -- -D warnings`; `git diff --check -- crates/engine/src/render/color_glyph.rs docs/renderer/final-local-implementation-closure.md docs/renderer/complete-algorithm-and-method-inventory.md docs/renderer/final-fallback-closure-report.md docs/renderer/final-fallback-inventory.md docs/renderer/final-universal-implementation-report.md docs/renderer/final-universal-renderer-implementation-report.md` |
| Malformed RawImage sampler hardening | Public sampler helpers could return opaque black for malformed `RawImage` shapes if called directly, although active painting rejected invalid decoded images before sampling. | `ImagePainter` now samples through `RawImage::pixel` and treats short buffers or unsupported channel counts as transparent no-sample output instead of opaque black. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine sample_short_buffer_is_transparent_not_black --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine sample_unsupported_channels_is_transparent_not_black --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine image_painter --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1 -- -D warnings` |
| Inline image metadata fail-closed | Inline images with missing or invalid `/Width` or `/Height` became zero-sized no-ops, non-mask inline images with missing or unsupported `/BitsPerComponent` defaulted to 8 or clamped, and non-mask inline images without a name-valued `/ColorSpace` defaulted to `/DeviceGray`, while image masks legitimately omitted color-space and bpc metadata. | `paint_inline_image` now records typed renderer refusals for missing/invalid dimensions, missing/unsupported non-mask bpc outside terminal JPX, and missing or non-name non-mask `/ColorSpace` entries before metadata planning/decode scheduling, and keeps `/ImageMask true` inline images on the valid 1-bpc stencil-mask `/DeviceGray` path. | `cargo fmt --all --check`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine renderer_inline_image_missing_width --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine renderer_inline_image_zero_height --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine renderer_inline_image_missing_bits_per_component --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine renderer_inline_image_unsupported_bits_per_component --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine renderer_inline_image_missing_color_space --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine renderer_inline_image_mask_without_color_space --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine inline_image --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1 -- -D warnings` |
| Image XObject metadata fail-closed | Image XObjects with missing or invalid `/Width` or `/Height` previously became `1x1` planner/cache inputs, non-mask XObjects with missing or unsupported `/BitsPerComponent` defaulted to 8 or clamped, and non-mask XObjects without valid `/ColorSpace` metadata could default to `/DeviceRGB`. | `handle_do_image` now records typed renderer refusals for missing/invalid dimensions, missing/unsupported non-mask bpc outside terminal JPX, and missing or malformed non-mask `/ColorSpace` metadata before image-reference/cache-key construction or decode planning. Terminal JPX remains the explicit exception because the codestream carries sample depth and color model metadata. | `cargo fmt --all --check`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine renderer_image_xobject_missing_width --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine renderer_image_xobject_ --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine image_xobject --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-engine --all-targets --jobs 1 -- -D warnings` |
| Image ColorSpace channel-count fail-closed | DeviceN image decode could default malformed component-name metadata to one channel, ICCBased image decode could default missing channel metadata to RGB before byte-count normalization/conversion, and Indexed base spaces could clamp malformed DeviceN/ICCBased component metadata before palette sizing. | `images/decoder.rs` now requires DeviceN image color spaces and Indexed DeviceN base spaces to resolve to a strict non-empty component-name array within the supported component limit, requires ICCBased image and Indexed ICCBased base channel metadata before normalization, palette sizing, or conversion, and `render/cmm.rs` requires explicit supported ICC profile `/N` metadata before ICC conversion. Malformed DeviceN and missing/invalid ICC metadata fail typed before decoded bytes are interpreted with guessed channel counts. | `cargo fmt --all`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine build_raw_image --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine device_n_image --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine icc_based_image --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine channel_clamp --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine cmm --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine tint --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture` |
| Calibrated image ColorSpace parameter fail-closed | Direct CalGray/CalRGB/Lab image conversion and Indexed calibrated base-palette conversion could synthesize default D50 white points, identity gamma/matrix, or default Lab ranges when the image color-space dictionary was missing or malformed. | `render/cmm.rs` now exposes checked calibrated image parameter readers, and `images/decoder.rs` uses them for direct image conversion and `ColorSpaceConverter` palette/base conversion. Missing parameter arrays, missing required `/WhitePoint`, malformed `/Gamma`, malformed `/Matrix`, invalid `/Range`, parameter references without a reader, and malformed Indexed calibrated base spaces fail typed before decoded bytes are converted with synthetic calibrated-space parameters. | `cargo fmt --all`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine image --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture` |
| Shading metadata fail-closed | Active shading validation could report missing `ShadingType` through an unsupported-type sentinel, while lower-level mesh decode/paint helpers could interpret a missing `ShadingType` as Type 4 or Type 6 and could flow missing Type 5 `/VerticesPerRow` through a zero row count before declining paint. | `page_renderer.rs` now requires `/ShadingType` before active named-shading and shading-pattern validation. `render/shading.rs` requires explicit mesh `ShadingType` values for `MeshDecode`, Gouraud mesh paint, and patch mesh paint; Type 5 paint also requires a finite row count of at least two before reading lattice rows. Mesh-stream lookup now declines missing `ShadingType` instead of using a zero sentinel. | `cargo fmt --all`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine mesh_decode --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine malformed_active_shading_dictionary_returns_typed_refusal --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine malformed_shading_pattern_returns_typed_refusal --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture` |
| Direct shading helper metadata fail-closed | `ShadingRenderer::paint_with_options` could be called directly without the page-renderer preflight, letting missing, malformed, or unsupported shading metadata reach lower-level defaults such as `/DeviceRGB` ColorSpace fallback or compatibility black/transparent color resolution. | `page_renderer.rs::validate_shading_dictionary_for_paint` is now crate-visible, and `render/shading.rs` invokes it at the direct paint boundary before dispatching any Type 1-7 shading helper. Direct helper calls now decline missing required metadata and unsupported color-space resolution instead of painting through defaulted shading fields. | `cargo fmt --all`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine direct_shading_paint_rejects_missing_color_space --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine direct_shading_helpers_reject_unsupported_color_space_locally --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine malformed_active_shading_dictionary_returns_typed_refusal --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine malformed_shading_pattern_returns_typed_refusal --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine --test shadings function_based_shading_type1_varies_with_x --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture` |
| SMask transfer-function shape fail-closed | SMask `/TR` transfer LUT construction probed the generic evaluator directly, so malformed supported function dictionaries could use evaluator defaults rather than failing closed. | `build_transfer_lut` now validates `/TR` with `validate_function_shape` before probing or building the LUT. Missing required function fields, unsupported functions, and evaluation failures return typed SMask transfer errors instead of silently using identity/default transfer behavior. | `cargo fmt --all`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine malformed_smask_transfer_function_returns_typed_refusal --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine unsupported_smask_transfer_function_returns_typed_refusal --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture` |
| PatternType required metadata fail-closed | Active pattern fill/stroke dispatch could report a missing `/PatternType` through an unsupported PatternType 0 sentinel. | `paint_pattern_fill` and `paint_pattern_stroke` now require `/PatternType` before dispatch. Missing pattern type records a typed missing-required-metadata refusal; unsupported numeric pattern types remain explicit unsupported-pattern refusals. | `cargo fmt --all`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine pattern_type_returns_typed_refusal --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture` |

| WASM premultiply BGRA lane path | The wasm32+`simd128` BGRA premultiply helper still loaded/stored vectors but extracted each four-pixel group into a temporary byte array for per-pixel scalar channel premultiplication before storing BGRA. | `render-simd` now shares the exact vector premultiply-RGBA lane helper between RGBA and BGRA output and emits BGRA with a SIMD channel shuffle plus scalar tails and scalar debug-oracle checks. | `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --jobs 1` with `RUSTFLAGS='-C target-feature=+simd128'`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd premultiply --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd public_kernels_match_scalar_or_cleanly_decline_unaligned_rows --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo fmt --all` |
| WASM grayscale lane expansion | Several wasm32+`simd128` grayscale conversion helpers loaded/stored vector groups but expanded GrayRGB, GrayRGBA, GrayBGRA, and premultiplied GrayRGBA through temporary byte arrays and scalar luma/channel loops. | `render-simd` now computes four-pixel luma through a shared SIMD helper, expands GrayRGB/GrayRGBA/GrayBGRA with SIMD shuffles, and feeds premultiplied GrayRGBA through the shared vector premultiply helper with scalar tails and scalar debug-oracle checks. | `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --jobs 1` with `RUSTFLAGS='-C target-feature=+simd128'`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd gray --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd public_kernels_match_scalar_or_cleanly_decline_unaligned_rows --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo fmt --all` |
| WASM Gray8 lane store | The wasm32+`simd128` Gray8 helper still stored four luma bytes by writing a full vector into a temporary array and copying the first four bytes. | `render-simd` now stores the low four luma bytes directly with a bounded wasm 32-bit lane store before scalar tails and scalar debug-oracle checks. | `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --jobs 1` with `RUSTFLAGS='-C target-feature=+simd128'`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd gray --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd public_kernels_match_scalar_or_cleanly_decline_unaligned_rows --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo fmt --all` |
| WASM RGB8 image-row expansion | The wasm32+`simd128` RGB8-to-opaque-RGBA helper still expanded four RGB pixels with a temporary byte array and scalar copy loop after loading the packed RGB group. | `render-simd` now reads each packed RGB group with bounded wasm zero-extending loads, combines the 12 source bytes with a SIMD shuffle, and expands to opaque RGBA with an alpha vector before scalar tails and scalar debug-oracle checks. | `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --jobs 1` with `RUSTFLAGS='-C target-feature=+simd128'`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd rgb --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd public_kernels_match_scalar_or_cleanly_decline_unaligned_rows --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo fmt --all` |
| WASM 3-byte row-group stores | GrayRGB, RGB, and BGR wasm32+`simd128` helpers still wrote 12-byte four-pixel row groups by storing a whole vector to a temporary array and copying the first 12 bytes. | `render-simd` now stores those low 12 SIMD bytes directly with bounded 64-bit plus 32-bit wasm lane stores before scalar tails and scalar debug-oracle checks. | `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --jobs 1` with `RUSTFLAGS='-C target-feature=+simd128'`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd rgb --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd gray --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd public_kernels_match_scalar_or_cleanly_decline_unaligned_rows --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo fmt --all` |
| Native SSSE3 3-byte row-group stores | Native RGB/BGR SSSE3 helpers still wrote 12-byte four-pixel row groups by storing a whole vector to a temporary array and copying the first 12 bytes. | `render-simd` now stores those low 12 SIMD bytes directly with the existing SSE2 low-lane store plus a shifted 32-bit lane write before scalar tails and scalar debug-oracle checks. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd rgb --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd public_kernels_match_scalar_or_cleanly_decline_unaligned_rows --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --lib --jobs 1`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --lib --jobs 1 -- -D warnings`; `cargo fmt --all` |
| Native SSSE3 RGB8 image-row expansion | Native RGB8-to-opaque-RGBA SSSE3 still staged each 12-byte source group in a temporary array before the shuffle expansion. | `render-simd` now reads each packed RGB group with a bounded SSE2 low-lane load plus a shifted 32-bit lane load, then expands to opaque RGBA with the existing SSSE3 shuffle and alpha vector before scalar tails and scalar debug-oracle checks. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd rgb --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd public_kernels_match_scalar_or_cleanly_decline_unaligned_rows --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --lib --jobs 1`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --lib --jobs 1 -- -D warnings`; `cargo fmt --all` |
| Native SSSE3 grayscale lane expansion | Native grayscale SSSE3 helpers still materialized four luma bytes into an array and expanded GrayRGB, GrayRGBA, and premultiplied GrayRGBA through scalar loops and temporary arrays. | `render-simd` now keeps four-pixel luma in a SIMD register, stores Gray8 through a low 32-bit lane write, expands GrayRGB and GrayRGBA with SSSE3 shuffles, and routes premultiplied GrayRGBA through the shared SSE2 premultiply group helper before scalar tails and scalar debug-oracle checks. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd gray --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd public_kernels_match_scalar_or_cleanly_decline_unaligned_rows --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --lib --jobs 1`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --lib --jobs 1 -- -D warnings`; `cargo fmt --all` |
| WASM SIMD RGB/BGR/BGRA channel rows | Simple RGB/BGR/BGRA caller-owned rows used scalar copy/reorder loops only. | `render-simd` exposes `rgba_to_rgb8`, `rgba_to_bgr8`, and `rgba_to_bgra8`; wasm32+`simd128` byte shuffles convert four-pixel groups, and the core row encoder calls them for non-grayscale, non-byte-reversed RGB/BGR rows and straight/opaque BGRA rows before scalar fallback. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`; `$env:RUSTFLAGS='-C target-feature=+simd128'; cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --jobs 1`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine contract_row_encoder --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1` |
| WASM SIMD copy/opaque RGBA rows | Straight and opaque RGBA caller-owned rows used scalar copy/alpha-force loops only. | `render-simd` exposes `copy_rgba` and `rgba_to_opaque_rgba`; wasm32+`simd128` direct loads/stores and byte shuffles convert four-pixel groups, and the core row encoder calls them for non-grayscale, non-byte-reversed straight/opaque RGBA rows before scalar fallback. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`; `$env:RUSTFLAGS='-C target-feature=+simd128'; cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --jobs 1`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine contract_row_encoder --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1` |
| CPU/WASM SIMD reverse-byte-order rows | Reversed caller-owned rows could only reach the scalar row encoder after the normal conversion kernels declined. | `render-simd` exposes `reverse_4byte_words_in_place`; x86 AVX2/SSE2, ARM NEON, and wasm32+`simd128` reverse RGBA/BGRA-sized words with a scalar oracle and tail, while the engine uses existing RGB/BGR channel-order kernels for 3-byte reversed rows, uses conversion-plus-reverse routing for straight/opaque/premultiplied/grayscale RGBA/BGRA rows, and on native hosts scalar-converts then reverse-word-routes 4-byte rows before final scalar fallback. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd reverse_4byte --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd public_kernels_match_scalar_or_cleanly_decline_unaligned_rows --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `$env:RUSTFLAGS='-C target-feature=+simd128'; cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --jobs 1`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine contract_row_encoder --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture` |
| WASM SIMD RGB image-span rows | Cached/scaled RGB image row expansion into opaque RGBA destination rows used scalar loops. | `render-simd` exposes `rgb8_to_opaque_rgba`; wasm32+`simd128` load/store groups expand RGB source triples into opaque RGBA rows with scalar-equivalence guards, and the unclipped and one-to-one binary-clipped RGB image row writers call it before scalar fallback. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`; `$env:RUSTFLAGS='-C target-feature=+simd128'; cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --jobs 1`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine opaque_rgb_run_writer --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1` |
| CPU/WASM SIMD common separable blend rows | Common separable opaque-source-over-opaque-destination blend rows used only the engine portable-wide path on native builds and lacked the direct wasm helper. | `render-simd` exposes `blend_separable_opaque_destination` for all PDF separable modes: Multiply, Screen, Overlay, Darken, Lighten, ColorDodge, ColorBurn, HardLight, SoftLight, Difference, and Exclusion; native x86/x86_64 SSE2 covers the full set with integer lanes for the integer byte formulas and float lanes for ColorDodge, ColorBurn, and SoftLight, wasm32+`simd128` uses true lane math for that full separable set, and the engine calls it before portable-wide fallback. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd blend_separable_opaque_dst_public_uses_sse2_when_available --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd separable --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd sse --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd public_kernels_match_scalar_or_cleanly_decline_unaligned_rows --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --lib --jobs 1`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --lib --jobs 1 -- -D warnings`; prior wasm32+`simd128` cargo check and Node smoke; focused engine separable tests |
| WASM SIMD soft-mask group-alpha rows | Soft-mask row dispatch rejected non-opaque group alpha before trying the wasm SIMD/native architecture kernels, so partial group opacity stayed on scalar engine fallback. | `render-simd` now computes the engine-compatible `src_alpha * mask * group_alpha / 255^2` effective alpha for soft-mask rows, accepts `group_alpha_255 < 255` in the public dispatcher, and the engine soft-mask opaque-destination row path uses the wide/SIMD path for non-opaque group alpha before scalar fallback. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd soft_mask --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd public_kernels_match_scalar_or_cleanly_decline_unaligned_rows --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine composite_from_soft_mask_fast_path_matches_opaque_destination_math --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `$env:RUSTFLAGS='-C target-feature=+simd128'; cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-render-simd --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --jobs 1` |
| Source-over mixed-alpha group-opacity rows | Normal Compat source-over rows with an opaque destination and partial group alpha used the wide uniform-alpha route only when every source pixel was fully opaque. Mixed source-alpha rows dropped into the general scalar compositor. | `buffer.rs` now uses a generalized opaque-destination uniform-alpha row path that scales each source pixel alpha by the byte-effective group alpha, processes two-pixel portable-wide groups, and uses an exact scalar tail for short rows. The runtime capability report names the mixed-source group-alpha opaque-destination lane. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine uniform_alpha --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine composite_from_ --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine renderer_capabilities_disclose_fallback_and_progressive_limits --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-engine --lib --jobs 1`; `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-engine --lib --jobs 1 -- -D warnings` |
| Source-over mixed-destination rows | Normal Compat source-over rows with non-opaque destination alpha fell through the general scalar compositor after opaque-destination fast paths declined. | `buffer.rs` now routes those rows through a portable `wide::f32x4` source-over compositor before scalar tails, preserving scalar source-alpha/group-alpha/destination-alpha math, RGB rounding, and alpha truncation. Runtime capabilities name `mixed_destination_source_over_f32x4`, and CLI compositor telemetry exposes `wide_general_pixels_delta`. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine composite_from_mixed_destination_general_row_uses_wide_path --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine "composite_from_" --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine renderer_capabilities_disclose_fallback_and_progressive_limits --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture` |
