| Soft-mask mixed-destination rows | Normal Compat soft-mask rows with non-opaque destination alpha fell through the scalar soft-mask general compositor. | `buffer.rs` now routes those rows through a portable `wide::f32x4` source-over compositor before scalar tails, using the scalar-compatible `soft_mask_effective_alpha` byte contract and preserving RGB rounding plus alpha truncation. Runtime capabilities name `soft_mask_mixed_destination_f32x4`, and CLI compositor telemetry exposes `wide_soft_mask_general_pixels_delta`. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine composite_from_soft_mask_mixed_destination_general_row_uses_wide_path --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine "composite_from_" --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine renderer_capabilities_disclose_fallback_and_progressive_limits --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture` |
| Render document cache budget | Byte-charged resource caches had independent caps, but the contract cache-byte budget did not bound their aggregate retained footprint and Form/tiling/annotation-appearance program, transformed path clip-node, font-map, glyph/mask, Type 3 parsed/raster artifacts, path-mask, and display-list caches were only partially byte-accounted. | `RenderDocumentCache::enforce_bounded_maps` now receives the active `RenderResourceBudget`, trims font/display/offscreen caps against it, and evicts byte-charged raw image, scaled image, SMask group, mesh-shading, Form-program, tiling-program, annotation-appearance program, transformed path clip-node, font-byte, font-resolver, glyph-outline, device glyph-mask, glyph-mask atlas page, Type 3 parsed geometry program, Type 3 parsed CharProc program, Type 3 mask, Type 3 rendered-glyph, path fill-mask, path stroke-mask, and retained display-list entries deterministically until their aggregate retained bytes fit `max_cache_bytes`; active ClipDag intern tables prune unreferenced nodes behind their node cap and return final per-render stats; transparent-page-group decisions use entry-capped LRU admission; glyph-mask atlas pages are byte-accounted inside the glyph mask cache and empty pages are released on eviction; Form/tiling/annotation-appearance program, path clip-node, font-map, glyph-atlas, Type 3 parsed program, display-list, and active ClipDag stats now report retained/cache pressure shape where applicable; runtime capability JSON and server `/api/v1/capabilities` expose structured renderer cache-pressure policy and the `render_document_cache_byte_accounting` reason names transformed path clip-node, glyph-outline, glyph-atlas, Type 3 parsed-program, Type 3 mask/rendered-glyph, and path-mask coverage; Rust `RenderContractTelemetryReport`, server multipart contract-report routes, and C/Python/WASM/.NET/Java source render-report APIs expose one-shot per-render cache counters including active ClipDag stats; CLI `render-corpus` reports retained display-list, annotation-appearance program, path clip-node, glyph-atlas, and active ClipDag cache stats. | `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine render_document_cache_enforces_aggregate_resource_budget --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine render_document_cache_byte_charges_path_clip_nodes --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine render_document_cache_byte_charges_glyph_outline_cache --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine render_document_cache_budget_evicts_raster_artifact_caches --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine render_document_cache_budget_evicts_type3_parsed_program_caches --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine type3_program_caches_report_byte_accounting --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine cache_evicts_least_recently_used_entry_by_byte_budget --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine render_document_cache_byte_charges_form_and_pattern_programs --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`; `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine render_document_cache_byte_charges_font_maps --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1`; Separable solid-fill mixed-destination rows are now covered in the same local
source pass: `buffer.rs` routes guarded separable `fill_rect` rows through
`blend_separable_src_over_run` and `blend_separable_src_over_partial_clip_run`;
opaque rows keep the existing optimized separable helpers, while
mixed-destination rows use portable `wide::f32x4` source-over math for source
alpha, clip alpha, destination alpha, RGB rounding, and alpha truncation.
Runtime capabilities name `separable_solid_mixed_destination_f32x4_rows`.
Evidence: `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine separable_fill_rect_mixed_destination --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`,
`cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine separable_ --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`,
and `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine renderer_capabilities_disclose_fallback_and_progressive_limits --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`.

| Packed backend plans | PARTIAL_ADVANCED | Vector packed plans exist and pre-resolve covered image/Form/shading/ExtGState/font/color-space/pattern/Properties resources; resource-aware Image XObject descriptors also carry first-level named image color-space payloads; BMC/BDC/EMC marked-content boundaries now force packed adapter replay so optional-content visibility suppresses vector clip/fill/stroke, including inline `/OC` dictionaries; contract full-page/tile renders can select named or indexed `/OCProperties /Configs` entries and inject the selected visibility context into packed and compatibility replay; supported Form XObject streams, including transparency-group interiors, now cache parent-resource-scoped retained plans and replay through the packed adapter; supported tiling-pattern streams now cache resource-scoped retained plans and replay through a tile-CTM-aware packed adapter; supported annotation appearance streams cache retained plans and replay through the packed adapter; Type 3 CharProcs with clips, typed resources, `d0`/`d1` metrics, and vector paths with per-paint inherited/explicit color handling can replay through guarded retained plans. Unsupported/advanced pattern subplans, richer interactive optional-content layer editing, complete transparency/group semantics, complete colour transforms, and complete text shaping/font-program state are not universally packed into backend-native payloads. |
| Retained immediate delegation | COMPLETE_ACTIVE_SOURCE | Default, cached, contract full-page, retained tile, direct tile, progressive supported-list rendering, and CLI `render-corpus --pipeline display-list` route through packed plans with typed descriptor replay and caller cancellation propagation. Compatibility and high-quality/exact retained page/display-list/tile/band/progressive/render-corpus unsupported-list paths refuse unsupported retained display-list replay with typed `UnsupportedFeature` instead of using raw dispatch or publishing immediate-render fallback tiles. SVG/PS tint-transform group-colour Form continuation note: vector Form classification now accepts structurally valid `/Separation` and one-/multi-component `/DeviceN` transparency-group `/CS` values in the same opaque-normal, vector-safe subset as device, calibrated, Indexed, and ICCBased group spaces. The classifier validates exact component metadata, rejects `/None` no-paint group spaces, and samples zero/full tint vectors through the named-color resolver before native SVG/PostScript replay. Evidence: `group_color_space_opaque_transparency_group_form_xobject_replays_natively`, engine check, and regional-vector clippy.

PostScript exact named-color shading continuation note: `render/vector_fallback.rs` now carries a PostScript-only named color-space sidecar for exact one-component Separation or DeviceN vector shadings whose tint transform can be serialized as a finite Type 2/Type 3 function, and pairs it with an exact one-component source shading function. `render/postscript.rs` emits the native `[/Separation ...]` or single-colorant `[/DeviceN ...]` color space in the `shfill` dictionary. SVG nonlinear tint transforms and multi-input/calculator DeviceN cases remain conservative. Evidence: `ps_output_resource_separation_nonlinear_tint_axial_shading_uses_exact_named_shfill`, `ps_output_resource_devicen_nonlinear_tint_axial_shading_uses_exact_named_shfill`, `svg_output_resource_separation_nonlinear_tint_axial_shading_stays_whole_page_raster`, `svg_output_resource_devicen_nonlinear_tint_axial_shading_stays_whole_page_raster`, and `ps_output_resource_devicen_multi_input_axial_shading_stays_whole_page_raster`.

SVG/PS regional order/bounds continuation note: SVG regional output now emits inert `data-wellfriend-region-kind` and `data-wellfriend-region-bounds` attributes, and PostScript output emits inert `% WellfriendRegion kind=... bounds=...` comments, for regional Image XObject and inline-image segments. Focused Image XObject and inline-image tests assert device-space bounds and verify each regional raster marker remains between surrounding native vector paths. Simple linear Type 2 vector shadings now also accept contained non-unit shading `/Domain` intervals by sampling native SVG/PS gradient endpoints at the domain endpoints; the focused synthetic gates cover axial SVG/PS output and the fallback boundary for domains outside the function domain. Accepted simple vector shadings now also preserve non-degenerate shading `/BBox` as a native SVG clipPath or PostScript clip around direct `shfill` and shading-pattern replay. Broader seam, transparency, pattern, non-linear/mesh shading, and corpus visual-parity work remains incomplete.

SVG/PS shading Extend continuation note: vector shading plans now retain the parsed `/Extend` pair. SVG classification accepts explicit, absent/default, false, and asymmetric extend pairs for otherwise vector-safe axial shadings and the SVG sink composes a device-space clipPath for the non-extended start/end half-planes before painting the native gradient. SVG classification also accepts finite concentric radial shadings with `r1 > r0` and non-default extend pairs, and the SVG sink composes native radial-gradient clips for the exact outer-circle, annular, or page-minus-inner-circle subset. PostScript classification accepts axial and radial non-default extend pairs because `shfill` carries native extend semantics, and the PostScript writer emits the exact pair for direct shadings and shading-pattern replay. Nonconcentric, reversed-radius, non-finite, or otherwise unsupported SVG radial non-default `/Extend` cases remain conservative. Focused evidence includes `nonextended_axial_shading_is_svg_and_postscript_native`, `absent_extend_axial_shading_defaults_to_nonextended_vector_flags`, `asymmetric_extended_axial_shading_keeps_vector_extend_flags`, `nonextended_concentric_radial_shading_is_svg_and_postscript_native`, `nonextended_nonconcentric_radial_shading_stays_conservative_for_svg`, `svg_output_nonextended_axial_shading_uses_clipped_native_gradient`, `svg_output_nonextended_concentric_radial_shading_uses_clipped_native_gradient`, `ps_output_nonextended_axial_shading_preserves_extend_flags`, `shading_bbox_clip_refuses_unflattenable_geometry`, and the `vector_fallback`/`radial_shading` focused gates.

SVG/PS colored/uncolored tiling-pattern continuation note: SVG and PostScript regional output now accepts simple colored and uncolored `/PatternType 1` path paints and pattern-painted inline/Image XObject stencil masks when the pattern stream decodes completely, carries exact finite geometry metadata, uses `/PaintType` 1 or 2, has positive tile steps, and its tile program is pure vector or contains scoped vector-safe image/Form/shading/inline-image resources without nested pattern paint. `/PaintType 2` additionally requires finite caller DeviceGray, DeviceRGB, or DeviceCMYK components and rejects tile streams that set paint color themselves. The sinks clip the painted path or stencil mask, apply per-tile BBox clips, and replay bounded tiles as native vector paths under `MAX_VECTOR_TILING_PATTERN_CELLS`. Nested pattern-paint, color-setting uncolored, negative-step, over-cap, malformed, transparency-bearing, resource programs outside the vector-safe image/Form/shading/inline-image subset, and advanced tiling-pattern cases remain conservative. Focused evidence includes `colored_tiling_pattern`, `uncolored_tiling_pattern`, `tiling_pattern_inline_image_mask`, `tiling_pattern_image_xobject_mask`, `resource_tile`, and the broader regional-vector `pattern` filter.

SVG/PS tiling-pattern text continuation note: simple colored and uncolored `/PatternType 1` glyph-outline text fills/strokes now use the same bounded native tiling replay as path paints. Focused evidence includes `tiling_pattern_text` and the broader regional-vector `pattern` filter.

SVG/PS classifier continuation note: stream/Form-local `Q` restore underflow and malformed `BT`/`ET` text-object sequences are now preflighted with malformed operand families before native vector sink replay.

SVG/PS vector text metric-source continuation note: native SVG/PostScript text replay now requires PDF glyph widths, strict sfnt/bare-CFF font-program advances, or valid vertical advances before moving the text matrix. The vector classifier refuses ordinary text when metric preflight is unavailable, and the SVG/PS sinks record typed output errors instead of using the raster-compatible `500` horizontal or `-1000` vertical metric defaults. A later local slice also makes visible/clipping SVG/PS glyph-outline emission require a real mapped glyph id from the font program, so PDF-provided widths no longer allow the sink to draw a code-point-derived compatibility glyph id. This closes a source-level vector text fallback boundary; it does not add broader shaping, unsupported font-program coverage, corpus validation, or external viewer proof.

Exact raster text metric-source continuation note: high-quality/exact raster text now shares the no-synthetic-metric policy at the active raster movement boundary. The compatibility renderer may still use the documented deterministic `500` ordinary-font fallback, but `HighQualityExact` raster rendering requires PDF widths, native Type 3 metrics, or strict variation-aware font-program advances, and vertical text must carry an explicit vertical advance.

Contract/progressive cancellation continuation note: normal and report-returning contract PNG/caller-owned buffer rendering now have source-visible cooperative cancellation handles and status queries across C/Python/WASM/.NET/Java, including Python bytearray caller-owned surface methods, WASM typed-object report variants, and WASM caller-owned buffer report variants. Progressive viewer-queue execution and adjacent-page prefetch execution have cancellable C/Python/WASM/.NET/Java source surfaces. Python cancellable contract PNG/report methods release the GIL when they do not hold Python-owned buffers; Python bytearray/progressive-job GIL behavior, single-thread JS hosts, native .NET/Java runtime smokes, browser/Node WASM smokes, and broader external binding runtime matrices remain incomplete. Compatibility methods remain non-cancellable by design.

Runtime fallback policy matrix continuation note: `RuntimeCapabilityReport::renderer_fallback_policies` now emits 20 machine-readable policy rows, including explicit typed-refusal rows for active pattern paint metadata, tiling-pattern required metadata, named-color metadata, active shading metadata, inline image metadata, Image XObject metadata, ExtGState render-state metadata, and active content operand/sequence refusals. The added rows do not increase material-degradation or canonical-immediate fallback counts.

Deterministic schedule continuation note: focused synthetic source tests now compare full-page output with single-tile, batched, and viewport-hinted progressive completion, a scoped two-worker progressive job matrix with different quantum schedules, plus display-list band stitching across multiple cold band heights, warm-cache crops from a cached full-page raster, alternating ownership across two separate worker-local `RenderDocumentCache` instances, and a scoped two-worker concurrent display-list band render with separate worker-local caches. These gates establish byte-identical RGBA output for the covered schedules only; corpus determinism, external viewer runtime determinism, broader server/external worker execution, hardware parity, benchmarks, and final visual parity remain incomplete.

SVG/PS named-paint continuation note: ordinary path fill/stroke/fill-stroke, text paint modes, Image XObject stencil masks, and inline stencil masks now preflight named color-space resources before native vector replay. Resource-named device paint remains vector-safe only for exact finite `/DeviceGray`, `/DeviceRGB`, or `/DeviceCMYK` components; unresolved, unsupported, invalid, or incomplete named paint now routes to the whole-page typed raster/refusal boundary instead of reaching the SVG/PS generic named-color black fallback.

SVG/PS ICCBased shading continuation note: resource-named Gray and RGB `/ICCBased` axial shadings now use strict profile `/N 1` or `/N 3` component matching and the existing CMM scalar conversion helper before native SVG/PostScript gradient emission. ICCBased vector shadings outside the guarded Gray/RGB profile shapes remain conservative and do not enter the regional native-gradient path.

Portable qcms CMM continuation note: the portable qcms ICC backend now admits only Gray/RGB profile shapes and rejects CMYK ICC profiles before qcms transform construction. Native LittleCMS remains the implementation path for CMYK ICC preview transforms; the portable backend records unsupported profile outcomes instead of panicking in debug builds.

Device-family component exactness continuation note: `ColorSpaceHandler::try_from_components` now rejects missing, overlong, or non-finite `/DeviceGray`, `/DeviceRGB`, and `/DeviceCMYK` vectors when callers have only a family name. Image SMask `/Matte`, luminosity SMask `/BC`, and SVG/PS named-paint preflight no longer reach padded, truncated, or defaulted device conversions in that no-dictionary path.

Image decode cache identity continuation note: inline-image decode cache keys now hash every payload byte and active render context instead of the first 128 bytes plus length, and the planner key now includes active render-contract identity, `ImageComponentSelection`, decoded target format, and backend identity so revision/contract, JPX/future component-subset, canonical RGBA, and backend-specific plans cannot share incompatible decoded output. Inline-image and Image XObject planning pass the active `RenderState` render-contract fingerprint into `ImageDecodePlanIdentity`, and cache strings expose a stable `:contract:` fragment. This does not claim native JPX ROI/region/progressive pixel output; guarded JPX downscale is target-resolution native reduction, while remaining JPX codec capabilities stay explicit limitations. Evidence: `cache_key_includes_render_contract_identity`, engine check, engine clippy, and workspace check.

Image decode execution-control capability continuation note: per-image capability reports now serialize cancellation and memory-budget control status, and the document-level report counts native cancellation, renderer-boundary cancellation, and renderer-boundary memory-budget coverage. Current codec adapters expose cancellation at the renderer boundary rather than as codec-native interruption, and memory budgets are enforced by decode scheduler reservations, adapter limits, and finalizer checks.

JPX metadata-inspection capability continuation note: `images/jpx.rs::inspect_metadata` parses JPX codestream/container headers through `hayro-jpeg2000` without decoding pixels and reports dimensions, original bit depth, color-space family, color/stored channel counts, and alpha state behind the existing decode-budget guard. `ImageDecodeCapabilityReport` now includes `metadata_inspection`, and `ImageDecodeCapabilityDocumentReport` counts `native_metadata_inspection_count`; JPX plans report metadata inspection as native while keeping ROI/region, codestream tile, component-subset, and progressive pixel output unavailable.

Monochrome terminal image decode guard continuation note: CCITT/JBIG2 terminal image decode now rejects non-`DeviceGray`/`G` declared or resolved image color spaces before full inline decode, Image XObject decode, inline CCITT source-window decode, or Image XObject CCITT source-window decode can hand monochrome samples to a multi-component renderer path. This is a fail-closed correction, not palette, calibrated, ICC, tint-space, ROI/reduction, or progressive color conversion for those terminal codecs.

JPX dimension-consistency continuation note: JPX terminal decode now uses the same checked finalizer for legacy inline decode, resolved-color-space inline decode, and Image XObject decode. The finalizer rejects decoded JPX dimensions that differ from PDF dictionary dimensions before `/SMaskInData` processing. This is a metadata fail-closed correction; JPX ROI/region/tile/component/progressive output remains an explicit unavailable capability at the current decoder API boundary; guarded target-resolution reduction is active where the selected decoder exposes exact native support.

JPX SMaskInData metadata continuation note: JPX terminal decode now validates `/SMaskInData` in the checked finalizer. Non-integer values, values outside `0`, `1`, and `2`, or `/SMaskInData 1`/`2` declarations without decoded alpha samples now fail typed before the image reaches downstream paint/cache behavior. This is a metadata fail-closed correction; JPX ROI/region, tile/component, progressive, and corpus coverage remain explicit unavailable or deferred evidence rather than faked output.

JPX finalizer invariant continuation note: JPX terminal decode now validates the normalized `RawImage` contract at the checked finalizer boundary. Non-8-bit sample output, zero-channel output, decoded byte-length mismatches, and decoded dimensions over the existing image decode budget fail typed before `/SMaskInData` processing or downstream paint/cache behavior. This is a boundary invariant correction; JPX ROI/region, tile/component, progressive, and corpus coverage remain explicit unavailable or deferred evidence rather than faked output.

JPX target-resolution reduction continuation note: guarded JPX downscale plans now use `hayro-jpeg2000` target-resolution decode for Image XObjects and inline images when no image mask or full-image postprocessing step requires the original sample grid. The reduced path validates the original JPX codestream dimensions against the PDF dictionary before accepting smaller decoded output. This adds native JPX reduction for the guarded downscale path; JPX ROI/region, tile/component, progressive, and cancellation-inside-codec stay explicit unavailable capabilities at the current decoder API boundary, and corpus coverage remains deferred.

DCT inline ColorSpace guard continuation note: legacy inline `DCTDecode` now reuses `finish_dct_decoded_image`, matching the resolved inline and Image XObject paths. Inline JPEG component counts are checked against the declared PDF image color space before raw output is returned, so mismatched declarations such as RGB JPEG data under `/DeviceCMYK` fail typed instead of painting plausible bytes with the wrong color semantics.

Image normalized-length continuation note: `build_raw_image` now validates exact normalized sample-buffer length immediately after bit-depth normalization and before decode arrays or color conversion. This prevents 8-bit CMYK and other converted image paths from reaching chunked color conversion with short or trailing malformed source bytes, and the DCT CMYK finalizer shortcut now applies the same decode-budget and exact-length boundary before conversion.

CMYK converter length continuation note: `ColorSpaceConverter::convert_with_options` now validates DeviceCMYK and fallback ICCBased-CMYK input length before calling the raw chunked CMYK-to-RGB helper. Short or trailing malformed CMYK buffers fail typed at the image conversion boundary instead of allowing `chunks_exact(4)` to ignore incomplete samples.

Color-glyph JPEG invariant continuation note: decoded JPEG color-glyph payloads now validate pixel cap, channel count, and exact byte length before returning `RawImage` output or converting CMYK bytes to RGB. This prevents malformed sbix/CBDT JPEG payloads from reaching chunked color conversion that could silently drop trailing bytes; it does not add new color-glyph payload formats or corpus font validation.

Color-glyph PNG invariant continuation note: decoded PNG color-glyph payloads now validate pixel cap, channel count, and exact byte length before returning `RawImage` output. Grayscale-alpha payloads are validated as two-channel decoded samples before `gray_alpha_to_rgba` expands them, preventing chunked conversion from dropping incomplete samples. This does not add new color-glyph payload formats or corpus font validation.

Color-glyph bitmap exactness continuation note: supported raw bitmap color-glyph decoders now require exact payload byte lengths for BGRA, gray8, gray subbyte, and mono formats before expansion. Overlong payloads fail typed instead of being truncated to the expected bitmap span; this does not add new bitmap formats or corpus font validation.

Sub-byte row exactness continuation note: decoded 1/2/4 bpc image data now must match the computed packed row-byte length exactly before `unpack_subbyte_rows` expands samples. Overlong buffers fail typed through `build_raw_image` before decode arrays, color conversion, or `RawImage` return; this does not add codec-region support or corpus validation.

Raw window source-length continuation note: unfiltered 1/2/4/8/16-bit source-window decode now validates the full raw source image byte length exactly before copying the requested window. Overlong inline-image or Image XObject raw source buffers fail typed instead of having trailing bytes ignored during crop; this does not add filtered source-window support, unsupported explicit-mask or SMask source-window support, reduction decode, non-axis partial decode, or corpus validation.

Raw window bit-depth continuation note: guarded axis-aligned unfiltered raw Image XObjects and inline images now use the same bounded source-window path for 1/2/4/8/16 bpc sample data. Byte-aligned 8/16 bpc windows copy row slices before existing normalization, and 1/2/4 bpc windows repack the requested samples into destination-local packed rows before `build_raw_image` applies the existing bit-depth normalization and color conversion. Focused evidence includes `raw_window`, `raw_unfiltered_subbyte_xobject_plan_uses_windowed_source_region_when_clipped`, and `renderer_capabilities_disclose_fallback_and_progressive_limits`. Filtered, unsupported explicit-mask/SMask, reduction, postprocessed, and non-axis raw windows remain typed unavailable.

Raw ImageMask source-window continuation note: guarded axis-aligned unfiltered 1 bpc `/ImageMask true` Image XObjects and inline images now use the bounded raw source-window path before stencil expansion. Image XObjects pass the cropped mask stream through dictionary-driven one-channel normalization, and inline masks reuse the DeviceGray 1 bpc path before `image_mask_to_stencil_rgba` applies `/Decode` polarity and fill color. Focused evidence includes `raw_window_xobject_decode_supports_image_mask_source_region`, `raw_window_unfiltered_image_mask_plan_uses_windowed_source_region_when_clipped`, `raw_window`, and `renderer_capabilities_disclose_fallback_and_progressive_limits`. Filtered masks, unsupported `/Mask`, `/SMask`, reduction, postprocessed, and non-axis raw windows remain typed unavailable.

Raw explicit Mask source-window continuation note: guarded axis-aligned unfiltered raw Image XObjects with color-key `/Mask` arrays or same-sized referenced 1 bpc stencil `/Mask` streams no longer force full-image materialization for clipped tiles. The renderer preflights referenced mask metadata, lets the planner admit a raw source region only for the crop-aligned subset, decodes the referenced stencil mask with the same raw source-window key as the main image, and then reuses the existing explicit-mask combiner on equal-sized cropped rasters. Focused evidence includes `raw_unfiltered_explicit_mask_plan_uses_windowed_source_region_when_crop_aligned`, `image_xobject_explicit_stencil_mask_uses_shared_raw_source_window_when_clipped`, and `image_xobject_color_key_mask_keeps_raw_source_window_when_clipped`. Filtered masks, non-stencil or mismatched referenced masks, `/SMask`, reduction, postprocessed, and non-axis raw windows remain typed unavailable.

Indexed lookup exactness continuation note: image `/Indexed` palette decoding and active/named Indexed color resolution now reject lookup byte strings whose length differs from `(hival + 1) * base_channels`. This prevents overlong palettes from being prefix-truncated before conversion through calibrated, tint, or device-family base spaces; it does not add new color-space support or corpus validation.

Indexed arity exactness continuation note: image `/Indexed` palette decoding and active/named Indexed color resolution now require the color-space array itself to have exactly four entries. Extra entries after lookup data fail typed instead of being ignored as inert metadata; this does not add new color-space support or corpus validation.

Calibrated arity exactness continuation note: CalGray, CalRGB, and Lab color-space arrays now require exactly two entries before image parameter parsing, active/named color conversion, or SVG/PostScript vector-shading classification. Extra entries after the parameter dictionary fail typed or keep vector output on the conservative fallback boundary; this does not add new CMM support or corpus validation.

Color-glyph sbix parser fail-closed continuation note: sbix strike metadata now uses typed parsing boundaries for strike headers, strike-offset tables, glyph-offset tables, glyph records, origin fields, duplicate glyph references, and pixels-per-em values. Truncated or malformed sbix records fail typed instead of being coerced to default metrics, converted to an unsupported dummy payload, or treated as absent color-glyph data; this does not add new sbix payload formats or corpus font validation.

PDF function input-arity continuation note: `eval_function_n` now validates Type 0, Type 2, Type 3, and Type 4 required shapes at the evaluator boundary and rejects missing or non-finite caller inputs before sampling. Type 0 no longer substitutes `0.0` for omitted dimensions, Type 2/3 no longer evaluate with absent caller input or missing required domain/encode metadata, and Type 4 executes only the strict `/Domain` input count; this does not add new function types or corpus validation.

Type 0/2/3 function evaluator-local continuation note: `render/function.rs::eval_type0` now enforces strict `/Size`, `/Domain`, `/Range`, present `/Encode`, and present `/Decode` metadata even when called below `eval_function_n`'s shape preflight. `render/shading.rs::eval_type2` and `eval_type3` now enforce their required `/Domain`, `/N`, `/Functions`, `/Bounds`, and `/Encode` fields locally and reject malformed present `/C0`/`/C1` arrays. Malformed arrays are no longer filtered into smaller grids, stitched segments, or component vectors, and malformed present encode/decode metadata no longer borrows default mappings. Evidence: `type0_evaluator_rejects_malformed_local_shape_fields`, `type2_evaluator_rejects_malformed_local_shape_fields`, `type3_evaluator_rejects_malformed_local_shape_fields`, and the `type0_`, `type2_`, and `type3_` focused gates.

Type 4 calculator output exactness continuation note: PDF Function Type 4 numeric operators now require finite numeric operands, `if`/`ifelse` require boolean conditions, and final outputs must be the top numeric stack values. Final booleans are no longer coerced to `0`/`1`, and final procedures are no longer filtered while earlier numeric values are kept. This closes malformed calculator tint/shading/transfer output coercion without adding new function operators, corpus validation, or benchmark evidence.

SMask transfer backdrop continuation note: alpha soft-mask `/BC` default-alpha probing now uses the same fail-closed transfer-function validation boundary before evaluating `TR(0)`. Unsupported, malformed, or empty-output `/TR` functions return typed errors instead of being interpreted as `TR(0)=0`; this does not add new transfer-function support or corpus transparency validation.

SVG shading BBox clip continuation note: SVG native shading, shading-pattern fill/stroke, and pattern-painted stencil replay now returns a typed unsupported error if the accepted shading `/BBox` cannot be materialized as a finite device-space clip. The previous replay path converted that failure into an empty clip and could silently omit paint. This does not add new native mesh, nonlinear shading, transparency, pattern, corpus, or benchmark coverage.

Overprint CMYK state exactness continuation note: active and retained display-list overprint-preview dispatch now requires an internal `DeviceCMYK` color state to carry exactly four finite components before using the CMYK preview painter. Malformed helper inputs decline the preview path instead of synthesizing missing inks as zero. Valid PDF color operators remain covered by the existing operand validators and valid CMYK preview behavior is unchanged; this does not add separation-preserving print output or native CMM proof coverage.

Annotation display-text exactness continuation note: synthesized annotation/widget display text now rejects non-finite internal real values before serializing generated appearance text, and the non-strict display helper no longer partially filters malformed array members while keeping earlier renderable text. Explicit `/Off` remains the accepted empty state. This closes the local appearance-synthesis helper boundary without adding corpus validation or external binding runtime gates.

Synthesized annotation helper-local exactness continuation note: the lower-level synthesized annotation helpers now enforce the same strict `/C`, `/CA`, `/QuadPoints`, `/L`, `/InkList`, and FreeText `/Q` invariants they previously relied on caller preflight to enforce. Direct helper use no longer clamps malformed color/opacity, expands malformed markup geometry to the full Rect, truncates line coordinates, filters bad quad/ink coordinates, emits partial ink path content, or defaults malformed FreeText alignment. Evidence: `synthesized_annotation_helpers_reject_malformed_metadata_locally` plus the existing public malformed annotation gates.

Vector `TJ` helper-local exactness continuation note: `render/vector_fallback.rs::text_operator_string_operands` now rejects direct `TJ` array members that are neither strings nor numeric kerning adjustments, even when reached below the shared active text preflight. Numeric integer and real kerning operands remain valid skipped items, while malformed names or other objects decline native vector text replay. Evidence: `tj_string_operand_collection_rejects_invalid_items`.

Synthesized widget helper-local exactness continuation note: `render/page_renderer.rs::synthesize_annotation_appearance` now runs the same widget metadata refusal boundary locally before no-author-appearance widget construction, `parse_default_appearance` returns no style for malformed present `/DA`, and `mk_rgb` requires exact finite unit `/MK /BG` and `/MK /BC` components. Direct helper use no longer clamps/defaults malformed widget chrome or text-style metadata below the public annotation preflight. Evidence: `synthesized_widget_helpers_reject_malformed_metadata_locally` plus the `widget_malformed` focused gate.

Utility image sample-length continuation note: `utilities.rs` now validates exact decoded sample lengths before image-to-PDF utility conversions expand PNG grayscale, grayscale-alpha, RGB, or RGBA data, before utility JPEG CMYK data is handed to the chunked CMYK-to-RGB helper, and before rendered-page RGBA/gray data is converted to RGB. This closes utility ingestion truncation cases without claiming new codec support, corpus coverage, or renderer-wide completion.

Raw source-window continuation note: unfiltered 1/2/4/8/16-bit raw Image XObjects and inline images now have a guarded axis-aligned source-window path that crops only the requested sample rows before `RawImage` construction; XObject output uses the planned source region in the cache key, and inline output reuses the same source-region CTM mapping. This does not claim filtered lossless, masked, reduced-resolution, postprocessed, or non-axis-aligned partial decode.

Image filter-chain capability exactness continuation note: image decode capability planning now treats any unknown filter in the filter chain as `UnknownCodec` instead of borrowing source-window or reduction capability from a later recognized terminal filter. This prevents unknown wrappers from inheriting guarded CCITT/raw/JPEG capability claims, while valid fully known CCITT chains still report native source-window support. This does not add new decoder APIs or corpus validation.

Active shading Domain sample exactness continuation note: active shading function sample-input generation now uses the strict shading float-array reader for present `/Domain` arrays and propagates malformed entries as typed shading validation errors. The previous helper could shorten arrays with `filter_map` and fall back to default sample inputs if reached outside the existing optional-Domain preflight. This closes a local validation-boundary gap without adding new shading types, corpus validation, or benchmark evidence.

PostScript exact shading-function continuation note: direct DeviceRGB/DeviceGray axial/radial shadings with a single finite Type 2 function remain native for PostScript/EPS by carrying the exact `/Domain`, `/N` exponent, and finite unit `/Range` metadata into a LanguageLevel 3 `/FunctionType 2` `shfill`; direct DeviceCMYK axial/radial shadings with a single finite Type 2 function now remain native as `/ColorSpace /DeviceCMYK` `shfill` with exact `/Domain`, `/C0`, `/C1`, `/N`, and finite unit `/Range`; direct DeviceRGB arrays of one-component Type 2 functions collapse to one exact RGB Type 2 sidecar when every component shares the same finite domain/exponent and the component ranges are all absent or exactly representable as a multi-output `/Range`, and otherwise serialize as exact per-component function arrays preserving finite component domains and ranges; direct DeviceCMYK arrays of one-component Type 2 functions do the same for `/ColorSpace /DeviceCMYK`; direct DeviceRGB/DeviceGray, Type 3 stitching functions whose Type 2 segments can be represented exactly as RGB now carry a native `/FunctionType 3` sidecar, preserving finite outer domains, segment `/N` exponents, and finite Type 2 domain/range metadata as well. SVG and calibrated/named, non-unit shading dictionaries, out-of-unit or malformed ranges, malformed domains, mesh, malformed, or otherwise unsupported nonlinear shadings remain fail-closed instead of being approximated.

PostScript exact Type 2 domain/range continuation note: `render/vector_fallback.rs` now accepts finite `/Domain` pairs and finite unit `/Range` arrays on direct DeviceRGB, DeviceGray, and DeviceCMYK Type 2 functions plus one-component Type 2 function arrays for the PostScript target, and `render/postscript.rs` serializes those domains/ranges into `/FunctionType 2` dictionaries for direct shadings, per-component function arrays, and Type 3 stitching segments. The focused source evidence is `nonlinear_type2_function_with_range_is_exact_postscript_regional`, `nonlinear_type2_function_with_non_unit_domain_is_exact_postscript_regional`, `nonlinear_device_rgb_type2_function_array_with_ranges_is_exact_postscript_regional`, `nonlinear_device_cmyk_type2_function_array_with_ranges_is_exact_postscript_regional`, `ps_rgb_shading_function_preserves_exact_type2_exponent`, `ps_rgb_shading_function_preserves_exact_type2_component_array`, `ps_cmyk_shading_function_preserves_exact_type2_component_array`, and `ps_cmyk_shading_function_preserves_exact_type3_stitching`; SVG remains conservative for nonlinear/ranged shadings.

PostScript exact DeviceCMYK stitching continuation note: direct `/DeviceCMYK` Type 3 stitching functions whose Type 2 segments are finite, unit-domain CMYK functions now carry native `/ColorSpace /DeviceCMYK` `/FunctionType 3` sidecars for PostScript/EPS, preserving non-linear segment exponents exactly. SVG and inexact/non-unit-domain stitching segments remain conservative.

Visual font metric exactness continuation note: visual text decoding now validates render-relevant simple `/Widths`, CID `/DW` and `/W`, and CID vertical `/DW2` and `/W2` metadata before glyph placement. Malformed entries, incomplete metric ranges, overlong/short simple width arrays, non-finite values, negative horizontal widths, and malformed vertical triples fail typed instead of being filtered, truncated, or defaulted into visible text advances. Standard 14 and spec-absent metric defaults remain accepted.

Active shading device-component exactness continuation note: `validate_device_color_components` now requires exact finite sampled outputs for `/DeviceGray`, `/DeviceRGB`, and `/DeviceCMYK` shading color spaces before active paint. Overlong outputs now record typed shading refusals instead of reaching a later truncating device conversion path.

Named-color component-vector exactness continuation note: `resolve_named_color_with_options` now rejects overlong component vectors for bare device resource names, CalGray/CalRGB/Lab named colors, Separation/DeviceN tint inputs, and device alternate outputs; `icc_components_to_srgb_with_options` now rejects ICCBased scalar vectors whose length differs from profile `/N`. This closes the remaining finite-prefix truncation paths in named paint and tint-space conversion, while full native CMM output remains incomplete.

SVG-in-OpenType color-glyph paint-metadata continuation note: static-subset `rgb()` components, `rgb()` percentages, and opacity attributes now reject out-of-range present values instead of clamping them into visible glyph paint. Omitted optional paint metadata still uses the SVG defaults.

SVG/PS regional Image XObject color-space preflight continuation note: regional Image XObject classification now resolves non-mask `/ColorSpace` metadata through page resources and applies the same vector-output decode support predicate used by inline images before choosing bounded SVG/PostScript embedding. Direct unsupported names, resource-named unsupported spaces, and malformed color-space arrays stay on the whole-page typed raster/refusal boundary instead of reaching late regional decode failure. Existing resource-named `/DeviceRGB` regional SVG and PostScript paths remain covered.

RV-14 convex-geometry continuation note: `render/path.rs` now routes finite, closed, single-subpath convex fills through a direct sampled-span compositor before building the general edge-bucket scanline table. The guard rejects non-finite, multi-subpath, degenerate, or concave geometry and falls back to the generic scanline path. Focused evidence includes `convex_polygon_fill_fast_path_matches_general_scanline` for byte-identical output against the generic scanline compositor and `concave_polygon_fill_rejects_convex_fast_path` for the rejection boundary.

Packed state-resource descriptor continuation note: resource-aware packed state compilation now emits typed refusals for missing `/ExtGState`, `/Font`, named non-device color-space, pattern color, and marked-content `/Properties` resources, including retained-text `/Tf` descriptors. Resource-free debug compilation remains permissive, but active resource-aware plans no longer retain those names for runtime lookup or allow vector replay to skip the refusal descriptor.

Packed text font-state continuation note: resource-aware packed compilation now tracks active pre-resolved text-font state across packed `q/Q`, refuses visible text-showing operators before any pre-resolved font is active, and pre-resolves well-formed ExtGState `/Font` selections into packed state descriptors. Packed replay snapshots active pre-resolved font/color/pattern resources with graphics-state save/restore, so `Q` restores the hot replay resource state instead of clearing it and forcing later resource-name lookup. Stale packed ExtGState `/Font` descriptors without pre-resolved font handles fail typed before mutating text state. Evidence: `packed_retained_text`, `packed_text_font_tracking_restores_unresolved_state_after_q`, `packed_ext_g_state`, `packed_plan_restore_restores_pre_resolved_active_font_resource`, `packed_plan_replay_refuses_unresolved_state_resource_descriptors`, and focused engine clippy.

Packed vector state/path arena continuation note: repeated identical vector paints now have a focused source guard proving packed path and DrawState arena interning share the same payload/state IDs across hot operations instead of duplicating path/state payloads. This closes the stale state-intern-table report claim for vector paint payloads while leaving resource-specific backend arenas partial. Evidence: `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine packed_plan_interns_repeated_vector_state_and_path_entries --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`.

All-features source-gate continuation note: the current all-features workspace check exposed a fuzz-feature compile drift where `fuzz_font_mapping` passed a `Cow<[u8]>` to `TextShaper::shape` after the shaping API required `&[u8]`. The fuzz path now borrows with `font_match.bytes.as_ref()`. Evidence: `cargo check --workspace --all-features --all-targets --jobs 1` initially failed with E0308, then passed after the fix; `cargo clippy --workspace --all-features --all-targets --jobs 1 -- -D warnings` also passed.

Type 3 retained exactness continuation note: Type 3 CharProc replay now fails closed in compatibility and high-quality/exact rendering when the nested retained CharProc plan is unavailable or contains a packed compile refusal, surfacing the retained refusal reason instead of dispatching the CharProc through immediate rendering. Full CharProc replay now returns failure when retained compile or execute records that fatal boundary instead of returning success after the typed error. Evidence: `high_quality_type3_charproc_refuses_unsupported_retained_replay` and `compatibility_type3_charproc_refuses_unsupported_retained_replay`.

Type 3 parsed-program lifecycle continuation note: parsed Type 3 CharProc and geometry caches now store explicit `Unvisited`, `Compiling`, `Compiled`, and `Failed` states with retained failure reasons. Cache misses mark an entry as compiling before source collection, then commit either compiled program data or failed state; child render-state absorption preserves those states under bounded LRU admission. Evidence: `type3_program_cache_tracks_explicit_lifecycle_states`, `type3_program_cache`, and `type3` focused gates.

Retained subprogram exactness continuation note: Form XObject, transparency Form group, annotation appearance, tiling-pattern cached programs, and soft-mask `/G` Form replay now preserve or compute retained-plan refusal reasons and use them as the retained replay boundary. Exact and compatibility rendering fail typed before immediate subprogram dispatch can run. Evidence: `unsupported_retained` focused gates.

Retained replay no-immediate continuation note: full-page display-list helpers and `render_page_display_list_tile_cancellable_with_mode_and_cache` now return `Result<PixelBuffer>` instead of `Result<Option<PixelBuffer>>`, and public tile, band, and progressive paths no longer carry a `None` branch that can invoke immediate tile rendering. The private immediate tile helper was removed, and progressive diagnostic test metadata now describes typed retained-tile refusal instead of canonical immediate tile publication.

SMask cache identity continuation note: `apply_smask` now resolves the SMask `/G` Form resource scope, validates transparency group `/CS`, and derives `/BC`/default-alpha backdrop identity before any cache lookup, so cached masks cannot bypass malformed, missing-resource, non-device group color-space, malformed backdrop, or malformed transfer-function refusals. `smask_group_cache_key` now includes the full structural clip fingerprint, explicit CMM and overprint policy fields, the resolved group color policy, deterministic `/G` resource fingerprint, and initial-backdrop/outside-alpha identity. This closes same-bounds/different-shape clip reuse plus group-policy/resource-scope/backdrop reuse holes in cached soft masks. Evidence: `smask_group_cache_key_includes_contract_output_identity` and the `smask` focused gate.

AcroForm widget invalidation continuation note: `document_subsystems.rs` now emits `document-subsystems-render-invalidation.v1` for FormData actions, including structured field/widget/AP write-set refs, created/changed/removed refs, affected pages, dirty widget rectangles for live appearance changes, and value/default deltas. `secure_mutation.rs` now emits `secure-mutation-incremental-form-render-invalidation.v1` for incremental form value updates and signature-preserving form fills, carrying field/widget/AP write-set refs, affected pages, dirty rectangles, and before/after values. Progressive server and C/Python/WASM/.NET/Java sessions apply render-invalidation plans to their owned caches, and non-progressive C/Python/WASM/.NET/Java source surfaces now expose caller-owned render cache handles for contract PNG/render-report paths. Evidence: `canonical_form_action_reopens_and_exposes_inverse_proof`, `document_subsystems`, and `secure_mutation_closeout_advanced_secure_mutation` focused gates.

Progressive binding/server cache-host invalidation continuation note: `ProgressiveRenderJob::apply_render_invalidation_plan_json` now applies `RenderInvalidationCachePlan` JSON to the session-owned `RenderDocumentCache`, invalidates affected retained current-page tile buffers, advances the progressive scheduler/publication identity, and reports obsolete tile-publication identities for stale-surface rejection. The server exposes this through owner-scoped `/api/v1/progressive/:id/apply-render-invalidation`; C/Python/WASM/.NET/Java expose the same mutation through their owned progressive session handles. This closes direct shared-cache mutation for progressive server and binding hosts; shared-resource transitive write sets, conservative resource-dependency narrowing, and external cache-handle runtime matrix validation remain incomplete. Evidence: `progressive_apply_render_invalidation_obsoletes_retained_tile_publication`, `capi_progressive_apply_render_invalidation_plan_obsoletes_retained_tile`, .NET build, and Java source compile.

Non-progressive render cache-handle continuation note: ordinary contract rendering now has caller-owned `RenderDocumentCache` handles outside progressive sessions. Rust engine contract PNG/render-report APIs accept a mutable cache, the C ABI/header expose `WellfriendRenderCache` creation, clearing, render-invalidation-plan application, and cached contract PNG/report entry points, and Python, WASM/TypeScript, .NET, and Java expose matching cache-owner surfaces. Render-report telemetry uses `scope: caller_owned_render_cache_report` on these paths. This closes the source-level non-progressive binding cache-handle adoption item; broader shared-resource transitive write-set matrices, safe narrowing of conservative page-level dependencies, and external runtime matrices remain incomplete. Evidence: `capi_render_cache_handle_renders_and_applies_invalidation_plan`, Python/WASM cargo checks, .NET build, Java source compile, and workspace check/clippy gates.

Knockout row-replacement continuation note: `PixelBuffer::knockout_from` now flattens existing knockout replacement semantics through bounded row slices instead of a per-pixel loop. The row helper preserves source RGB replacement, source/group/soft-mask/clip-scaled alpha, clip-zero skips, and zero-soft-mask transparent replacement RGB, and exposes `knockout_row_pixels` through core/CLI compositor telemetry. This closes the source hot path for the existing knockout replacement primitive without claiming full PDF knockout/backdrop/group-colour-space closure. Evidence: `knockout_from_fuses_partial_clip_and_soft_mask_into_row_path`, focused engine check/clippy, and focused CLI check/clippy.

Transitive retained resource-op tile dependency continuation note: retained tile dependency recording now walks bounded transitive source references for intersecting XObject/Form, named-shading, and active retained state-resource objects before converting them to canonical source IDs. A tile that intersects a Form XObject records both the Form source object and nested resource objects declared in the Form resource dictionary; text/font, fill/stroke color-space, ExtGState, and pattern paints likewise record nested descriptor/function/SMask/resource refs when the operation intersects the tile. Edits to those nested sources can invalidate the exact recorded tile without falling back to full-page raster invalidation. Source-only mapped transactions and render-invalidation plans with no reported affected pages now use the existing page-artifacts-plus-exact-tiles path; it keeps exact source tiles when recorded tile coverage is complete and still expands source-derived pages when coverage is incomplete. Broader transaction write-set classification across arbitrary shared resources, remaining conservative page-level narrowing, and external runtime matrices remain incomplete. Evidence: `transitive_`, `source_dependency`, `bounded_form_xobject`, `transaction_invalidation`, and `exact_tile_mode` focused gates.

Transaction write-set binding alias continuation note: nested render write-set extraction now matches field names and structured object-reference keys across snake_case, camelCase, kebab-case, and case differences. Binding/server JSON using names such as `changedFontRefs`, `changedOutputIntentRefs`, `changedPageTreeRefs`, `changedCatalogRefs`, `changedStructTreeRefs`, `changedTransparencyGroupRefs`, `changedImageSmaskRefs`, `renderWriteSetRefs`, `fontObject`, `fontGeneration`, `objectRef`, and `sourceRef` maps to the same canonical source IDs and source-cache marker derivation as the snake_case envelopes. The typed plan extractor and `RenderInvalidationCachePlan` decoder now also accept binding-style direct plans and `report.renderInvalidation` envelopes using `schemaVersion`, `nextRevision`, `mappedSourceIds`, `sourceCacheMarkers`, `sourceId`, `objectNumber`, `generationNumber`, `affectedPages`, `affectedTiles`, `pageNumber`, and `conservativeResetRequired`. The public `TransactionWriteSet` serde path accepts binding-style affected object/page/tile/revision fields and both tuple/object affected-tile shapes, and dirty-region tile conversion accepts binding-style page/bounds aliases such as `pageNumber` and `dirtyRegion`. Unknown/unmapped refs still trigger the conservative reset boundary. Evidence: `nested_write_set_collector_accepts_camel_case_binding_refs`, `nested_write_set_collector_accepts_document_structure_and_group_aliases`, `render_invalidation_plan_json_accepts_camel_case_plan_fields`, `render_invalidation_plan_json_accepts_camel_case_envelope_and_tile_fields`, `transaction_write_set_json_accepts_camel_case_fields_and_tile_objects`, `dirty_regions_accept_camel_case_page_and_bounds_aliases`, `transaction_invalidation`, `nested_write_set_collector`, `render_invalidation_plan_json_`, engine check, and engine clippy.

Transaction display/proof/prepress alias continuation note: nested render
write-set extraction now also recognizes display/print/proof OutputIntent refs,
display/proof/proofing profile refs, color-management profile and policy refs,
CMM profile refs, Separation/DeviceN profile refs, rendering intent,
transfer-function, halftone-screen, overprint state/mode, prepress policy,
prepress plate, ink/spot color, Separation/DeviceN plate, black-point
compensation, black-generation, undercolor-removal, trapping, and trap-network
refs through explicit changed-ref aliases and structured object/generation keys.
Known refs map to canonical source IDs and source-cache markers before cache
application; unknown refs keep the existing conservative reset behavior.
Evidence:
`nested_write_set_collector_accepts_display_proof_profile_aliases`,
`nested_write_set_collector_accepts_prepress_overprint_aliases`,
`transaction_invalidation`, engine check, and engine clippy.

JPX alias decode-planning/vector-fallback continuation note: `/JPX` is now handled as the same terminal image codec as `/JPXDecode` in image decode planning, lossless stream stopping, decoder terminal dispatch, inline target-resolution dispatch, DecodeParms matching, codec-isolation backend selection/reporting, and SVG/PostScript vector-fallback image filter classification. Abbreviation-bearing JPX images keep guarded target-resolution reduction/capability reporting, backend reporting, and regional vector image handling instead of being classified as `UnknownCodec` full-decode-only, unsupported codec registry, or whole-page vector fallback. JPX ROI/region/progressive output is not exposed by the current decoder API and remains reported unavailable rather than faked. Evidence: `jpx_abbreviation`, `image_decode_planning`, and `jpx` focused gates.

Image decode component-selection capability continuation note: component-subset image decode requests now participate in the active planner/refusal model instead of only salting cache identity. `ImageDecodePlan` records `requires_component_decode`, `ImageDecodeCapabilityReport` exposes `component_decode`, exact-mode limitation summaries include `component-selection`, and the document-level capability JSON reports `native_component_decode_count`. The raw unfiltered adapter now implements selected-component source-window output for Image XObjects and inline images, including full-window raw component requests, packed 1/2/4-bit samples, 8/16-bit samples, and `/Decode` mapping by original source component index. Known source shapes normalize explicit all-component selections so they do not require subset support, invalid known raw component indexes fail exact planning instead of being advertised as native, and per-image capability reporting marks shape-supported raw plus monochrome CCITT source-window and trivial single-component support as native instead of staying filter-only conservative. JPEG/JPX/JBIG2 and filtered-lossless adapters still report unavailable native component-subset decode; CCITT reports the trivial single-component selection as native for 1 bpc DeviceGray/ImageMask shapes; JPX ROI/region/tile/progressive pixel output remains reported unavailable at the current decoder API boundary. Evidence: `component_selection`, `raw_component`, `image_decode_planning`, `component_subset_plan_fails_exact_when_native_component_decode_is_unavailable`, and `image_decode_capability_report_lists_images_without_decoding`.

Image decode codestream-tile capability continuation note: codec-native tile decode now participates in image capability reporting instead of remaining an implicit JPX limitation. `ImageDecodeCapabilityReport` exposes `tile_decode`, the document-level capability JSON reports `native_tile_decode_count`, and JPEG/JPX plans report `CodestreamTileDecodeUnavailable` when the selected decoder API cannot return codestream tiles natively. This does not claim native JPX tile pixel output; it exposes the refusal beside region, reduction, progressive, and component decode. Evidence: `jpx_plan_reports_full_decode_only_capability`, `jpx_downscale_plan_reports_native_reduction_capability`, `image_decode_capability_report_lists_images_without_decoding`, and `image_decode_planning`.

Image decode CCITT terminal-shape exactness continuation note: the active planner, image-reference capability report, and runtime capability text now use the same terminal monochrome shape boundary as the decoder before advertising native CCITT source-window support. CCITT source-window capability remains native for 1 bpc DeviceGray/G or image-mask shapes, but CCITT streams declared as RGB/CMYK now report `MonochromeTerminalShapeUnsupported`, keep a full source-region cache identity, and make `HighQualityExact` refuse source-region-required tiles instead of planning a native region path that the decoder would later reject. Evidence: `ccitt_plan_does_not_advertise_window_decode_for_non_monochrome_shape`, `ccitt_image_reference_capability_reports_non_monochrome_shape`, `image_decode_planning`, and `renderer_capabilities_disclose_fallback_and_progressive_limits`.

Proof OutputIntent selection continuation note: `engine.rs::first_output_intent_profile_bytes` now scans catalog `/OutputIntents` candidates for the first dictionary with `DestOutputProfile` instead of stopping at an unprofiled first entry. Present malformed or image-filtered profile streams still fail closed; native CMM runtime validation and proof/separation execution remain incomplete. Evidence: `proof_output_intent_profile_scan_skips_unprofiled_entries`.

Print separation surface validation continuation note: `render/print_profile.rs` now validates print/prepress policy against an explicit `PrintOutputSurfaceKind`, and `PageRenderer::prepress_plate_report` validates the production plate-report path against the `SeparationFramebuffer` surface before dispatching page content. Normal RGB/gray render contracts still refuse `OverprintPolicy::PreserveSeparations`, while the prepress N-channel surface accepts preserved separations as the explicit source output path. RGB ordered halftone plus preserved separations remains typed unsupported, and `Proof` still requires `NativeLittleCms`. Evidence: `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine print_profile --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`, `cargo test -p wellfriendpdf-engine prepress_plate_report --lib --jobs 1 -- --test-threads=1 --nocapture`, `cargo check -p wellfriendpdf-engine --lib --jobs 1`, and `cargo clippy -p wellfriendpdf-engine --lib --jobs 1 -- -D warnings`.

Visual normalization pixel-policy continuation note: the compact renderer visual-diff harness now applies explicit render-context RGBA background compositing, grayscale luma conversion, exact post-rotation dimension validation, and packed raw byte-order normalization to the canonical comparison surface. It also canonicalizes structured font-environment sidecars into stable strings plus SHA-256 fingerprints so equivalent manifests do not create false font-fallback classifications while genuine fallback/provider changes remain classified. The manifest documents these active policy steps, and per-side normalization/context reports carry the expected size, applied byte order, applied background/grayscale flags, and `font_environment_fingerprint`. This closes the harness-side source gap where those fields were only policy metadata; real corpus comparison, profile adjudication, and human visual review remain deferred. Evidence: `python -m pytest tools/renderer-visual-diff/test_visual_normalization.py -q`, `python -m py_compile tools/renderer-visual-diff/visual_diff.py`, and `python -m json.tool tools/renderer-visual-diff/normalization-manifest.json`.

Render-contract color-scheme continuation note: `engine.rs` now applies
`ColorScheme::Dark` and `ColorScheme::ForcedMonochrome` to active CPU contract
output after proof and halftone processing. Dark mode inverts RGB while
preserving alpha; forced monochrome converts RGB to deterministic contract luma
while preserving alpha before either canonical `PixelBuffer` return or
caller-owned row encoding. The old generic semantic-policy refusal was replaced
with a typed field-list refusal, so unsupported policy requests identify their
divergent fields. Later local slices activate non-default smoothing policies and
`ResearchHybrid` CPU hybrid dispatch rather than silently mapping those contract
fields away; remaining unsupported policy combinations still fail typed.

ExtGState paint-dependent semantic continuation note: well-formed `/SA`, `/AIS`,
and `/TK` values now enter `GraphicsState`, are saved/restored, and participate
in retained `DrawState` plus packed plan identity. Visible paint that would
observe unsupported stroke-adjustment, alpha-source, or disabled text-knockout
semantics now fails typed at active, retained, and packed replay paint
boundaries; non-observing cases such as `/SA true` fill-only paint continue to
render. This closes the prior metadata-time over-refusal and retained/vector
silent-paint gap without claiming implementation of those PDF semantics. The
focused retained text replay gate now covers `/TK false`, `/AIS true`, and
stroked-text `/SA true` paint refusals alongside the direct renderer. Focused
evidence: `extgstate_`, `try_apply_ext_gstate`, `packed_vector_plan`, and engine
library check.

Retained display-list raw-operation storage continuation note: retained
display-list compilation no longer keeps cloned raw `ContentOperation` values
for path assembly or pending inline-image state. It tracks path assembly with a
typed count, stores only inline-image parameter operands while waiting for the
data payload, and accounts typed retained payloads directly instead of building
temporary raw operation vectors. Retained replay payloads remain typed
`DisplayOp` variants; parser-level and SVG/PostScript sink `ContentOperation`
boundaries remain separate source/sink concerns. Evidence: the `native` focused
gate, engine library check, engine library/test clippy, format check, and the
display-list source guard for removed raw-operation storage patterns.

Image capability full-decode-only continuation note: static image capability
reports now use an explicit full-decode-only classifier instead of treating
every full-source/full-resolution capability report as incapable. Raw and CCITT
image references with native region or component capability are therefore not
counted as full-decode-only in document reports, while progressive image decode
sessions still report `full_decode_required` for nonterminal full-source work
without native progressive continuation. Evidence:
`image_decode_capability_report`,
`progressive_full_raw_plan_reports_full_decode_required_even_with_window_capability`,
`progressive_image_decode_session_reports_full_decode_required`, and the
`native` focused gate.

Image DecodeParms cache-identity continuation note: decoded-image cache identity
now includes stable filter-parameter semantics. `ImageMetadata`,
`ImageContractState`, and `ImageDecodeCacheKey::to_cache_string` carry a
`decode_params_fingerprint`; active inline-image planning fingerprints
normalized inline `/DecodeParms` operands, and Image XObject planning
fingerprints `/DecodeParms` or `/DP` from the stream dictionary. Otherwise
identical image payload/filter/base identities with different predictor, CCITT,
JBIG2, or other filter-parameter semantics therefore cannot share decoded
pixels. Evidence: `cache_key_includes_decode_params`,
`cache_key_differs_for_decode_params`, the `image_decode_planning` focused gate,
engine check, and engine clippy.
